Pass-boundary gates come first; output-kind gates follow in output\n// order. A contract is included once because the v2 environment is a set of\n// semantic declaration tuples; expected_outputs preserves output multiplicity.\n// This includes evidence gates intentionally not wired on the verification\n// pass itself.\nfunc (s *Session) applicableGateIDs(pass PassSpec, expected []backend.ExpectedOut) ([]string, error) {\n\tvar gateIDs []string\n\tseen := map[string]bool{}\n\tadd := func(gateID string) {\n\t\tif gateID == \"\" || seen[gateID] {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[gateID] = true\n\t\tgateIDs = append(gateIDs, gateID)\n\t}\n\tfor _, gateID := range pass.Gates {\n\t\tadd(gateID)\n\t}\n\tfor _, output := range expected {\n\t\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[output.Kind]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: expected output kind %s is not cataloged\", output.Kind)\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\t\tadd(gateID)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tfor _, gateID := range gateIDs {\n\t\tif _, ok := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]; !ok {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: applicable gate %s is not cataloged\", gateID)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn gateIDs, nil\n}\n\nfunc hashBytes(body []byte) string {\n\tsum := sha256.Sum256(body)\n\treturn hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])\n}\n\nfunc environmentEntriesValue(entries []backend.EnvironmentEntry) []any {\n\tout := make([]any, 0, len(entries))\n\tfor _, entry := range ([]backend.ExpectedOut, error) {\n\traw, ok := manifest[\"expected_outputs\"].([]any)\n\tif !ok || len(raw) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: v2 Run Manifest has no expected outputs\")\n\t}\n\toutputs := make([]backend.ExpectedOut, 0, len(raw))\n\tdeclared := map[string]bool{}\n\tfor i, value := range raw {\n\t\tentry, ok := value.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: expected_outputs[%d] is not an entry\", i)\n\t\t}\n\t\trequired, requiredPresent := entry[\"required\"].(bool)\n\t\toutput := backend.ExpectedOut{\n\t\t\tOutputID: str(entry, \"output_id\"), Identity: str(entry, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\tKind: str(entry, \"kind\"), Placement: str(entry, \"placement\"),\n\t\t\tKindSchemaVersion: uint16(num(entry, \"kind_schema_version\")), Required: required,\n\t\t}\n\t\tderived, present := entry[\"derived_from_outputs\"].([]any)\n\t\tif !present {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: expected_outputs[%d] has no derivation closure\", i)\n\t\t}\n\t\toutput.DerivedFromOutputs = make([]string, 0, len(derived))\n\t\tfor j, value := range derived {\n\t\t\tid, ok := value.(string)\n\t\t\tif !ok || id == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: expected_outputs[%d].derived_from_outputs[%d] is invalid\", i, j)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\toutput.DerivedFromOutputs = append(output.DerivedFromOutputs, id)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif output.OutputID == \"\" || output.Identity == \"\" || output.Kind == \"\" || output.Placement == \"\" || output.KindSchemaVersion == 0 || !requiredPresent {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: expected_outputs[%d] is incomplete\", i)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif declared[output.OutputID] {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: !declared[dependency] || dependencies[dependency] {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: expected_outputs[%d] has invalid derivation %q\", i, dependency)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdependencies[dependency] = true\n\t\t}\n\t\tdeclared[output.OutputID] = true\n\t\toutputs = append(outputs, output)\n\t}\n\treturn outputs, nil\n}\n\nfunc environmentEntry(entries []backend.EnvironmentEntry, kind, id string) (backend.EnvironmentEntry, bool) {\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.Kind == kind && entry.ID == id {\n\t\t\treturn entry, true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn backend.EnvironmentEntry{}, false\n}\n\n// envApplicabilityMemo caches the content-pure half of a semantic-environment\n// applicability verdict, keyed by the run manifest hash: everything the\n// verdict derives from immutable, content-addressed store objects. The two\n// inputs that can move under a live Session stay checked on every hit: the\n// session-resident declaration bodies (catalog contracts, environment world,\n// check registry) are re-hashed against the memoized entries, and the\n// decision-revocation probe re-runs against the caller's state.\ntype envApplicabilityMemo struct {\n\tentries []backend.EnvironmentEntry\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) manifestSemanticEnvironmentApplicable(state *State, manifest map[string]any) bool {\n\thash, hashErr := store.RunManifestHash(manifest)\n\tif hashErr == nil {\n\t\tif memo, ok := s.envApplicability[hash]; ok {\n\t\t\tif s.sessionEnvironmentCurrent(memo.entries) {\n\t\t\t\treturn !s.anyEnvironmentDecisionRevoked(state, memo.entries)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdelete(s.envApplicability, hash)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tconsistent, entries := s.semanticEnvironmentConsistent(manifest, s.getObject)\n\tif !consistent {\n\t\t// Only affirmative verdicts are stable for a session: every input of\n\t\t// a true verdict is immutable, while a missing object can still be\n\t\t// admitted later. Negatives re-evaluate.\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif hashErr == nil {\n\t\tif s.envApplicability == nil {\n\t\t\ts.envApplicability = make(map[string]envApplicabilityMemo)\n\t\t}\n\t\ts.envApplicability[hash] = envApplicabilityMemo{entries: entries}\n\t}\n\treturn !s.anyEnvironmentDecisionRevoked(state, entries)\n}\n\n// sessionEnvironmentBody resolves the session-resident source body a declared\n// environment entry is minted from. Entries whose sources are\n// content-addressed or compiled in (target-state bodies, sandbox profiles)\n// are immutable by construction and report resident=false.\nfunc (s *Session) sessionEnvironmentBody(entry backend.EnvironmentEntry) ([]byte, bool) {\n\tswitch entry.Kind {\n\tcase \"pass-contract\":\n\t\treturn s.Catalog.PassBodies[entry.ID], true\n\tcase \"gate-contract\":\n\t\treturn s.Catalog.GateBodies[entry.ID], true\n\tcase \"decision-record\":\n\t\treturn s.Environment.Decisions[entry.ID], true\n\tcase \"policy\":\n\t\treturn s.Environment.Policies[entry.ID], true\n\tcase \"check-registry\":\n\t\tif s.Checks == nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, true\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn s.Checks.Raw, true\n\tcase \"aliasing-registry\":\n\t\treturn s.Environment.AliasingRegistry, true\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n}\n\n// sessionEnvironmentCurrent reports whether every session-resident source\n// body still hashes to its memoized entry pin, so a memo hit covers exactly\n// the declarations the full evaluation covers.\nfunc (s *Session) sessionEnvironmentCurrent(entries []backend.EnvironmentEntry) bool {\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tbody, resident := s.sessionEnvironmentBody(entry)\n\t\tif !resident {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif len(body) == 0 || hashBytes(body) != entry.Hash {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// manifestSemanticEnvironmentApplicableUncached is the lock-held witness\n// variant: it reads every body directly from the Graph Store and consults no\n// session memo, so a decide-before-seal admission check can never observe\n// cached state (writer-side checks stay uncached and fail closed).\nfunc (s *Session) manifestSemanticEnvironmentApplicableUncached(state *State, manifest map[string]any) bool {\n\tconsistent, entries := s.semanticEnvironmentConsistent(manifest, s.Graph.GetObject)\n\tif !consistent {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn !s.anyEnvironmentDecisionRevoked(state, entries)\n}\n\n// semanticEnvironmentConsistent evaluates the content-pure half of the\n// applicability verdict through the given read function and returns the\n// manifest's environment entries for the caller's live probes (source-body\n// currency and decision revocation).\nfunc (s *Session) semanticEnvironmentConsistent(manifest map[string]any, read func(string) ([]byte, error)) (bool, []backend.EnvironmentEntry) {\n\tif intOf(manifest, \"schema_version\") < 2 {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tpassValue, ok := manifest[\"pass\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tpass, ok := s.Catalog.Passes[str(passValue, \"id\")]\n\tif !ok || intOf(passValue, \"contract_version\") != pass.ContractVersion || intOf(passValue, \"impl_version\") != pass.ImplVersion {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tstepValue, ok := manifest[\"planned_step\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tstep := planStepFromMap(stepValue)\n\tif step.PassID != pass.ID {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\texpectedOutputs, err := expectedOutputsFromManifest(manifest)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\ttargetValue, ok := manifest[\"target\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\ttargetProjection, err := targetmodel.ProjectionFromMap(targetValue)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\ttargetBody, err := read(targetProjection.Declaration.ContentHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\twant, err := s.resolveExactEnvironment(pass, step, expectedOutputs, &targetProjection, targetBody)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tgot, err := environmentEntriesFromManifest(manifest)\n\tif err != nil || len(got) != len(want.Entries) {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tfor i := range want.Entries {\n\t\tif got[i] != want.Entries[i] {\n\t\t\treturn false, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tbody, err := read(got[i].Hash)\n\t\tif err != nil || hashBytes(body) != got[i].Hash {\n\t\t\treturn false, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\twantGateIDs, err := s.applicableGateIDs(pass, expectedOutputs)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\trawGateIDs, ok := manifest[\"gate_ids\"].([]any)\n\tif !ok || len(rawGateIDs) != len(wantGateIDs) {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tfor i, gateID := range wantGateIDs {\n\t\tif value, ok := rawGateIDs[i].(string); !ok || value != gateID {\n\t\t\treturn false, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true, got\n}\n\n// anyEnvironmentDecisionRevoked is the live half of the applicability\n// verdict: decision revocation moves during a session, so it re-evaluates\n// against the caller's state on every call, memoized or not.\nfunc (s *Session) anyEnvironmentDecisionRevoked(state *State, entries []backend.EnvironmentEntry) bool {\n\tif state == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.Kind != \"decision-record\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tidentity := state.Identities[entry.ID]\n\t\tif identity != nil && identity.Head != 0 && identity.Versions[identity.Head] != nil &&\n\t\t\tidentity.Versions[identity.Head].ContentHash == entry.Hash &&\n\t\t\tstate.DecisionRevoked(entry.ID, identity.Head, entry.Hash) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) manifestPinsCurrentGate(manifest map[string]any, gateID string) bool {\n\tif intOf(manifest, \"schema_version\") < 2 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tspec, ok := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\tif !ok || spec.PredicateVersion < 1 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tbody := s.Catalog.GateBodies[gateID]\n\tif len(body) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tfoundID := false\n\trawGateIDs, _ := manifest[\"gate_ids\"].([]any)\n\tfor _, raw := range rawGateIDs {\n\t\tif value, ok := raw.(string); ok && value == gateID {\n\t\t\tfoundID = true\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !foundID {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\trawEntries, _ := manifest[\"environment\"].([]any)\n\twantHash := hashBytes(body)\n\tfor _, raw := range rawEntries {\n\t\tentry, ok := raw.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok || str(entry, \"kind\") != \"gate-contract\" || str(entry, \"id\") != gateID || str(entry, \"hash\") != wantHash {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tstored, err := s.getObject(wantHash)\n\t\treturn err == nil && hashBytes(stored) == wantHash\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc dispatchVerificationSpec(manifest map[string]any, manifestHash string) (*backend.VerificationSpec, error) {\n\tprojection, ok := manifest[\"verification\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\trootHash := str(projection, \"root_run_manifest_hash\")\n\tif rootHash == \"\" {\n\t\trootHash = manifestHash\n\t}\n\tregistryMap, registryOK := projection[\"registry\"].(map[string]any)\n\tprofileMap, profileOK := projection[\"profile\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !registryOK || !profileOK {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: verification projection lacks exact registry/profile\")\n\t}\n\tdecodeEntry := func(value map[string]any) backend.EnvironmentEntry {\n\t\treturn backend.EnvironmentEntry{Kind: str(value, \"kind\"), ID: str(value, \"id\"), Hash: str(value, \"hash\"), Path: str(value, \"path\")}\n\t}\n\trawOccurrences, _ := projection[\"occurrences\"].([]any)\n\toccurrences := make([]backend.CheckOccurrence, 0, len(rawOccurrences))\n\tfor i, raw := range rawOccurrences {\n\t\tvalue, ok := raw.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok || intOf(value, \"root_ordinal\") != i {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: verification occurrence %d is invalid\", i)\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey, err := checks.OccurrenceKey(rootHash, i)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\toccurrences = append(occurrences, backend.CheckOccurrence{\n\t\t\tOccurrenceKey: key,\n\t\t\tRootOrdinal:   i,\n\t\t\tCheckID:       str(value, \"check_id\"),\n\t\t\tCheckHash:     str(value, \"check_hash\"),\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\trawClaims, _ := projection[\"claimed_root_ordinals\"].([]any)\n\tclaims := make([]string, 0, len(rawClaims))\n\tfor _, raw := range rawClaims {\n\t\tordinal := intOfValue(raw)\n\t\tif ordinal < 0 || ordinal >= len(occurrences) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: verification claim ordinal %d is outside root universe\", ordinal)\n\t\t}\n\t\tclaims = append(claims, occurrences[ordinal].OccurrenceKey)\n\t}\n\treturn &backend.VerificationSpec{\n\t\tRootRunManifestHash:   rootHash,\n\t\tOccurrences:           occurrences,\n\t\tClaimedOccurrenceKeys: claims,\n\t\tRegistry:              decodeEntry(registryMap),\n\t\tProfile:               decodeEntry(profileMap),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// environmentDigest is useful in witnesses and reports without changing the\n// declaration order that preserves exact multiplicity.\nfunc environmentDigest(entries []backend.EnvironmentEntry) string {\n\tvalues := make([]string, 0, len(entries))\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tvalues = append(values, entry.Kind+\"\\x00\"+entry.ID+\"\\x00\"+entry.Hash)\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(values)\n\treturn hashBytes([]byte(strings.Join(values, \"\\n\")))\n}\n\n// LoweringRuleContext is the exact declared lowering-rule-set context a\n// planning session lowers under: one entry per declared catalog lowering rule,\n// each pinning the rule's identity, route, status, and exact body hash, plus a\n// set digest over the whole. It is recorded verbatim into plan and run\n// provenance (the plan_record and its dispatched pass_run_opened) so a later\n// reader can prove which lowering rules governed a plan without re-reading the\n// catalog. It carries no composition arithmetic: it only names the rules that\n// were in force, never how any demand composed. An empty rule set yields the\n// zero value (Digest == \"\"), which callers omit from provenance so a rule-free\n// plan is byte-identical to one written before lowering rules existed\n// (work-graph-integrated@2, clause subject-assembly-is-deterministic).\ntype LoweringRuleContext struct {\n\tDigest string\n\tRules  []LoweringRuleContextEntry\n}\n\n// LoweringRuleContextEntry pins one declared lowering rule at the grain plan\n// and run provenance record it: its authored id, the pass it routes a matched\n// demand to, its lifecycle status, and the hash of its exact declaration body.\ntype LoweringRuleContextEntry struct {\n\tID        string\n\tRoutePass string\n\tStatus    string\n\tBodyHash  string\n}\n\n// Empty reports whether the context names no declared lowering rule, so a\n// caller can omit it from provenance and leave a rule-free plan unchanged.\nfunc (c LoweringRuleContext) Empty() bool { return c.Digest == \"\" }\n\n// loweringRuleContext derives the declared lowering-rule-set context from the\n// loaded catalog rules. Entries keep the rules' authored order; the digest is\n// order-independent (sorted over exact per-rule tuples) so it identifies the\n// set of rules in force, not the accident of load order. An empty rule set\n// returns the zero value.\nfunc loweringRuleContext(rules []LoweringRule) LoweringRuleContext {\n\tif len(rules) == 0 {\n\t\treturn LoweringRuleContext{}\n\t}\n\tentries := make([]LoweringRuleContextEntry, 0, len(rules))\n\tdigestParts := make([]string, 0, len(rules))\n\tfor _, rule := range rules {\n\t\tbodyHash := hashBytes(rule.Body)\n\t\tentries = append(entries, LoweringRuleContextEntry{\n\t\t\tID:        rule.ID,\n\t\t\tRoutePass: rule.RoutePass,\n\t\t\tStatus:    rule.Status,\n\t\t\tBodyHash:  bodyHash,\n\t\t})\n\t\tdigestParts = append(digestParts, rule.ID+\"\\x00\"+rule.RoutePass+\"\\x00\"+rule.Status+\"\\x00\"+bodyHash)\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(digestParts)\n\treturn LoweringRuleContext{\n\t\tDigest: hashBytes([]byte(strings.Join(digestParts, \"\\n\"))),\n\t\tRules:  entries,\n\t}\n}\n\n// provenanceValue renders the context as the exact ledger payload sub-object a\n// plan_record and a pass_run_opened record carry under \"lowering_context\".\nfunc (c LoweringRuleContext) provenanceValue() map[string]any {\n\trules := make([]any, 0, len(c.Rules))\n\tfor _, entry := range c.Rules {\n\t\trules = append(rules, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"id\":         entry.ID,\n\t\t\t\"route_pass\": entry.RoutePass,\n\t\t\t\"status\":     entry.Status,\n\t\t\t\"body_hash\":  entry.BodyHash,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn map[string]any{\n\t\t\"digest\": c.Digest,\n\t\t\"rules\":  rules,\n\t}\n}\n\n// loweringRuleContextFromValue decodes the \"lowering_context\" sub-object a fold\n// reads back from a plan_record or pass_run_opened payload. An absent or\n// malformed value folds to the zero (rule-free) context.\nfunc loweringRuleContextFromValue(value any) LoweringRuleContext {\n\traw, ok := value.(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn LoweringRuleContext{}\n\t}\n\tctx := LoweringRuleContext{Digest: str(raw, \"digest\")}\n\trules, _ := raw[\"rules\"].([]any)\n\tfor _, item := range rules {\n\t\tentry, ok := item.(map[string]any)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tctx.Rules = append(ctx.Rules, LoweringRuleContextEntry{\n\t\t\tID:        str(entry, \"id\"),\n\t\t\tRoutePass: str(entry, \"route_pass\"),\n\t\t\tStatus:    str(entry, \"status\"),\n\t\t\tBodyHash:  str(entry, \"body_hash\"),\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn ctx\n}\n\nfunc backendAliasingClass(declarations []scheduler.Declaration, backendID string) string {\n\tfor _, declaration := range declarations {\n\t\tif declaration.BackendID == backendID {\n\t\t\treturn declaration.AliasingClass\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\nfunc workspaceFromManifest(manifest map[string]any, fallbackEffect string) (backend.WorkspaceSpec, error) {\n\tfallback := backend.WorkspaceSpec{\n\t\tEffectClass: workspaceEffect(fallbackEffect),\n\t\tWriteScope:  backend.WriteScope{Allow: []string{\"**\"}, Deny: []string{}},\n\t}\n\tif intOf(manifest, \"schema_version\") < 2 {\n\t\treturn fallback, nil\n\t}\n\tvalue, ok := manifest[\"workspace\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn backend.WorkspaceSpec{}, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: v2 Run Manifest lacks workspace contract\")\n\t}\n\tscope, ok := value[\"write_scope\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn backend.WorkspaceSpec{}, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: v2 Run Manifest lacks write scope\")\n\t}\n\treturn backend.WorkspaceSpec{\n\t\tEffectClass: str(value, \"effect_class\"),\n\t\tWriteScope: backend.WriteScope{\n\t\t\tAllow: stringsOf(scope, \"allow\"),\n\t\t\tDeny:  stringsOf(scope, \"deny\"),\n\t\t},\n\t}, nil\n}\n",
    "internal/driver/fold.go": "package driver\n\nimport (\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/records\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/scheduler\"\n\t\"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/store\"\n\ttargetmodel \"github.com/halbritt/striatum-next/internal/target\"\n)\n\n// State is the driver's fold of the ledger: everything the loop decides\n// against. It is derived and destroyable; sessions may resume a compatible,\n// graph-bound snapshot and apply the verified ledger tail.\ntype State struct {\n\tRequests map[uint64]*RequestState\n\tRuns     map[uint64]*RunState\n\t// runsByManifestHash is the append-ordered lookup used by verification\n\t// reports. It replaces an O(all runs) search for every historical report;\n\t// nil means a test assembled State directly and callers must fall back to\n\t// the public Runs projection.\n\trunsByManifestHash map[string][]*RunState\n\tIdentities         map[string]*IdentityState\n\t// admittedReceiptHashCounts is the content-addressed membership projection\n\t// for versions whose identity's current kind is Receipt. Counts preserve the\n\t// fold's historical final-kind semantics across repeated content and the\n\t// (degraded but readable) case where one identity changes kind. Report\n\t// closure checks use it instead of rebuilding the same set from every\n\t// identity for every report.\n\tadmittedReceiptHashCounts map[string]int\n\tGateResults               map[gateKey][]GateResultState\n\t// gateOperations is the derived global operation-key index for every v2\n\t// gate_result, including records whose contextual force is invalid. It is\n\t// append-order-sensitive because exact replay returns the latest duplicate.\n\tgateOperations map[string]gateResultOperationState\n\tEscalations    map[uint64]*EscalationState\n\t// ImportedSubmissions records v2 seam durability only. It prevents the\n\t// disposable spool copy from being reopened after submission_received or\n\t// submission_refused, without claiming that the dispatch is drained.\n\tImportedSubmissions map[string]bool\n\t// Drained is the historical v1 drain projection. V2 completion is the\n\t// journal-derived dispatch_effects_complete predicate in the Graph Store.\n\tDrained           map[string]bool\n\tSubmissions       map[string]SubmissionState\n\tMarkedWaves       map[uint64]bool\n\tHeadMovementWaves map[uint64]HeadMovementWaveState\n\tStalenessChunks   map[uint64]map[int]bool\n\tPlans             map[uint64]*PlanState\n\t// plansBySeq retains every exact plan occurrence so historical producing\n\t// runs can prove their D0006.C12 plan authority after a newer request plan\n\t// replaces the latest-plan projection above.\n\tplansBySeq           map[uint64]*PlanState\n\tSchedulingDecisions  map[uint64][]SchedulingDecisionState\n\tHeadMovements        []HeadMovementState\n\tFlakes               map[string]bool\n\tApplications         []ApplicationState\n\tIntegrationConflicts []IntegrationConflictState\n\tTreeMovedDiagnostics []TreeMovedDiagnosticState\n\tWinners              map[string]uint64\n\tRevocations          map[string][]RevocationState\n\tResolutions          map[uint64]*ResolutionState\n\tLastDriveSession     DriveSessionState\n\tLastSeq              uint64\n\t// LastCapacityReleaseSeq is the latest first closure of a lane-bound run.\n\t// A release invalidates an earlier scheduler saturation deferral: the\n\t// scheduler must be allowed to evaluate the changed in-flight set instead\n\t// of sleeping until a horizon computed from the former occupants.\n\tLastCapacityReleaseSeq uint64\n}\n\n// DriveSessionState is the latest durable drive-session observation. uint64\n\tReplacementRequestRef uint64\n\tRedirectPin           *store.Pin\n\tEffectOperationKey    string\n\tResolver              string\n\tNote                  string\n\tEffective             bool\n}\n\n// DecisionRevoked answers whether the exact decision body is withdrawn.\n// Legacy identity-only records remain readable history, but cannot withdraw a\n// v2 decision version whose exact content hash they never named.\nfunc (state *State) DecisionRevoked(identity string, versionSeq uint64, contentHash string) bool {\n\tfor _, revocation := range state.Revocations[identity] {\n\t\tif false\n}\n\n// PlanningActive distinguishes an ordinary request from a dormant replacement\n// that has not been selected by its superseded request's exact cancellation.\nfunc (request *RequestState) PlanningActive() bool {\n\treturn request != nil && (!request.Dormant || request.ActivatedBy != 0)\n}\n\n// SubmissionState is the folded intake fact for one drained submission bundle.\ntype SubmissionState struct {\n\tDispatchID string\n\tRunRef     uint64\n\tStatus     string\n}\n\n// ApplicationState is one application_record: a change set bound into\n// product lineage.\ntype                uint64\n\tTargetSemanticHash            string\n\tTargetProjectionHash          string\n\tTargetProjectionObjectHash    string\n\tTargetProjectionObjectAddress E2 operation. It never licenses a head\n// movement; it only preserves the failed operation's contextual identity so a\n// bounded restage/rebuild cannot borrow a body hash from another scope.\ntype TreeMovedDiagnosticState struct {\n\tSeq                uint64\n\tOperationKey       string\n\tGateResultRef      uint64\n\tGateOperationKey   string\n\tStagedProduct      store.Pin\n\tProducingRunRef    uint64\n\tRunManifestHash    string\n\tRequestRef         uint64\n\tTargetSemanticHash string\n\tExpectedBase       map[string]any\n\tCurrentBase        map[string]any\n\tChangeSets         []store.Pin\n\tDetail             string\n\tInvalidReason      string\n}\n\n// exactTreeMovedDiagnostic returns the latest applicable diagnostic for an\n// exact Change Set version. Identity, version, and content hash are all\n// load-bearing; equality of bytes alone has no force. Multiple distinct failed\n// gates are history, so the latest exact operation is the recovery context.\nfunc (s *State) exactTreeMovedDiagnostic(pin store.Pin) *TreeMovedDiagnosticState {\n\tvar found *TreeMovedDiagnosticState\n\tfor index := range s.TreeMovedDiagnostics {\n\t\tdiagnostic := &s.TreeMovedDiagnostics[index]\n\t\tif diagnostic.InvalidReason != \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tmatched := false\n\t\tfor _, candidate := range diagnostic.ChangeSets {\n\t\t\tif candidate == pin {\n\t\t\t\tmatched = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !matched {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif found == nil || diagnostic.Seq > found.Seq {\n\t\t\tfound = diagnostic\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn found\n}\n\n// HeadMovementState is one recorded head movement, for the mark phase.\ntype HeadMovementState struct {\n\tSeq           uint64\n\tSchemaVersion uint16\n\tIdentity      string\n\tMovement      string\n\tFrom          uint64\n\tTo            uint64\n\tOperationKey  string\n\tEffect        map[string]any\n\tGateResults   []uint64\n}\n\n// HeadMovementWaveState is the exact deterministic invalidation consequence\n// selected from the ledger prefix ending at one head movement. Keeping the\n// selection in the fold lets later chunks validate against the immutable\n// cause prefix rather than recomputing from a newer world.\ntype HeadMovementWaveState struct {\n\tCauseRef     uint64\n\tOperationKey string\n\tDigest       string\n\tChunkSize    int\n\tChunkCount   int\n\tCause        map[string]any\n\tBasis        map[string]any\n\tSubjects     []any\n}\n\ntype gateKey struct {\n\tIdentity string\n\tVersion  uint64\n\tGateID   string\n}\n\n// RequestState is one compilation request's authority events.\ntype RequestState struct {\n\tSeq           uint64\n\tSchemaVersion uint16\n\tSubject       string\n\tPrincipal     string\n\tNote          string\n\tTargetID      string\n\tTargetVer     int\n\t// TargetProjection exists only for a valid v2 request. Legacy is the\n\t// non-lossy degraded variant \u2014 a v1 request, or a v2 request whose pinned\n\t// projection fails the strict decode: its original schema and payload\n\t// survive verbatim, and no absent authority or target context is invented.\n\tTargetProjection *targetmodel.Projection\n\tLegacy           *LegacyRequestState\n\tProgressOnly     bool\n\tInvalidReason    string\n\t// Payload is the exact v2 compilation_request payload. Run planning copies\n\t// it into the canonical Run Manifest so constraints and issuer context are\n\t// never reconstructed from a lossy fold projection.\n\tPayload map[string]any\n\t// DelegationRef marks an agent-issued request (RFC 0006 delegation);\n\t// WrittenAt supports issuance cadence accounting.\n\tDelegationRef string\n\tWrittenAt     string\n\t// IssuerKind is the folded issuer.kind \u2014 \"planner\" distinguishes a\n\t// planner-scheduled request from a Principal- or agent-issued one\n\t// everywhere the driver reads folds (C9). A record predating the marker\n\t// folds as its recorded kind; an absent kind folds as principal-issued \u2014\n\t// no retroactive migration, ever (C13).\n\tIssuerKind string\n\t// Dormant replacements remain planning-inactive until the exact old\n\t// request cancellation effect names them. Supersedes is immutable request\n\t// context, never inferred from a current target.\n\tDormant     bool\n\tSupersedes  uint64\n\tActivatedBy uint64\n\tSatisfied   bool\n\t// SatisfactionLevel is the claim level the satisfaction earned from its\n\t// conjunct evidence (Verified/Asserted/Designed) \u2014 a floor-only satisfaction\n\t// is Asserted, never a bare Verified (D0005.C5 on the satisfaction surface).\n\tSatisfactionLevel string\n\tSatisfactionRef   uint64\n\tCanceled          bool\n\tCancellationRef   uint64\n}\n\n// LegacyRequestState preserves a degraded compilation request exactly: a\n// pre-v2 record, or a v2 record whose pinned target projection fails the\n// strict decode. It is readable history, never a source of synthesized\n// current authority, and never a source of an executable projection.\ntype LegacyRequestState struct {\n\tSchemaVersion uint16\n\tPayload       map[string]any\n}\n\n// recordedTargetSemanticHash returns the semantic hash string the degraded\n// request record itself carries, without decoding the projection. Authority\n// linkage (satisfaction, cancellation) compares these recorded strings\n// exactly; an absent recorded hash matches nothing.\nfunc (request *RequestState) recordedTargetSemanticHash() run and its closures.\ntype RunState struct {\n\tSeq                   uint64\n\tSchemaVersion         uint16\n\tPassID                string\n\tStepID                string\n\tSubject               string\n\tRequestRef            uint64\n\tPlanRef               uint64\n\tPlanStepHash          string\n\tDeadlineClass         string\n\tManifestHash          string\n\tManifest              map[string]any\n\tOpenedAt              time.Time\n\tClosures              []string\n\tClosureSources        []string\n\tClosureRefusalCodes   []string\n\tClosureDetails        []string\n\tRetryAt               time.Time\n\tSchedulingDeferralSeq uint64\n\n\t// Lane binding facts (from lane_binding records): the ledger-derived\n\t// basis for in-flight accounting (D0013.C6) and courtesy aborts.\n\tBackendID             string\n\tDispatchID            string\n\tLaneID                string\n\tAttempt               int64\n\tDeclarationHash       string\n\tIndependencePartition string\n\tLaneBindings          []LaneBindingState\n\n\t// LoweringContext is the exact declared lowering-rule-set context this run\n\t// was dispatched under, folded verbatim from the pass_run_opened record.\n\t// Zero (Digest == \"\") for a rule-free run.\n\tLoweringContext LoweringRuleContext\n}\n\n// LaneBindingState preserves every append rather than letting a later\n// conflicting binding hide an earlier one in the derived fold.\ntype LaneBindingState struct {\n\tSeq           uint64\n\tPayload       map[string]any\n\tBinding       scheduler.LaneBindingPayloadV1\n\tInvalidReason string\n}\n\n// SchedulingDecisionState preserves a scheduler invocation exactly. V2\n// output preimages are the sole recovery authority for a missing binding;\n// legacy decisions remain readable but cannot synthesize that authority.\ntype SchedulingDecisionState struct {\n\tSeq           uint64\n\tSchemaVersion uint16\n\tRunRef        uint64\n\tPayload       map[string]any\n\tInvalidReason string\n}\n\n// Open reports whether the run has no closure.\nfunc (r *RunState) Open() bool {\n\treturn len(r.Closures) == 0\n}\n\nfunc (r *RunState) SchedulingDeferred() bool {\n\tfor _, source := range r.ClosureSources {\n\t\tif source == \"scheduling_deferral\" {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// IdentityState folds one artifact identity: versions, head, staleness.\ntype IdentityState struct {\n\tID       string\n\tKind     string\n\tRole     string\n\tVersions map[uint64]*VersionState\n\tOrder    []uint64\n\tHead     uint64\n}\n\n// VersionState is one admitted artifact version.\ntype VersionState struct {\n\tSeq            uint64\n\tContentHash    string\n\tKind           string\n\tLifecycle      string\n\tProducedByRun  uint64\n\tProductionMode string\n\tAnchors        map[string]string\n\tRetiredAnchors []store.RetiredAnchor\n\tDerivedFrom    []store.Pin\n\tConstrainedBy  []store.Pin\n\tEdges          []store.Edge\n\tBodyAddress    string\n\tStale          bool\n\n\t// Evidence mirror: review-ledger versions carry their subject pin and\n\t// verdict in the admitted record's evidences edge, so gate and re-stamp\n\t// folds never open bodies.\n\tReviewVerdict         string\n\tReviewSubjectIdentity string\n\tReviewSubjectVersion  uint64\n\tReviewSubjectHash     string\n\n\t// Foreign-head mirror: a Foreign Head Attestation (an Exogenous Change\n\t// Record, D0014.C2) carries the attested foreign accepted head in its\n\t// evidences edge \u2014 subject = the foreign head, claim = \"foreign_head:<hash>\".\n\t// Mirrored here so cross-repository pin freshness reads a local head plus a\n\t// hash comparison, never a foreign graph and never a body (D0014.C3).\n\tForeignContentHash string\n\n\t// SessionNonce, BackendID, and AliasingClass mirror the producing\n\t// lane's admitted attribution \u2014 what independence evaluation reads.\n\tSessionNonce  string\n\tBackendID     string\n\tAliasingClass string\n}\n\n// GateResultState is one recorded gate result.\ntype GateResultState struct {\n\tSeq              uint64\n\tSchemaVersion    uint16\n\tGateID           string\n\tClass            string\n\tPredicateVersion int\n\tOutcome          string\n\tVerdict          string\n\tDetail           string\n\tEvidencePins     []store.Pin\n\tOperationKey     string\n\tRequestRef       uint64\n\tTargetSemantic   string\n\tProducingRunRef  uint64\n\tRunManifestHash  string\n\tGateContractHash string\n\tPayload          map[string]any\n\tInvalidReason    string\n}\n\ntype gateResultOperationState struct {\n\tpayloadHash string\n\tlatestSeq   uint64\n\tconflict    bool\n}\n\n// matchGateResultOperation preserves the raw-ledger replay rule: an exact\n// payload replays the latest duplicate, while any unequal record sharing the\n// operation key makes the operation conflicting forever.\nfunc (state *State) matchGateResultOperation(operationKey string, payload map[string]any) (uint64, bool) {\n\tentry, ok := state.gateOperations[operationKey]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n\tpayloadHash, err := store.RunManifestHash(payload)\n\tif err != nil || entry.conflict || entry.payloadHash != payloadHash {\n\t\treturn 0, true\n\t}\n\treturn entry.latestSeq, false\n}\n\n// EscalationState is one escalation and its resolution state.\ntype EscalationState struct {\n\tSeq                  uint64\n\tReason               string\n\tBudgetKind           string\n\tDetail               string\n\tBlockingScope        string\n\tCandidatePin         store.Pin\n\tRequestRef           uint64\n\tRunRef               uint64\n\tPayloadHash          string\n\tRelatedResolutionRef uint64\n\tResolutionRef        uint64\n\tResolved             bool\n\tDisposition          string\n}\n\n// Blocks reports whether an unresolved escalation still carries blocking\n// force over fresh work. An escalation bound to a terminal request \u2014\n// canceled or satisfied \u2014 is moot for execution: the request it was blocking\n// can no longer act, and a reissued successor starts with its own budget and\n// its own escalations. It stays visible and unresolved for the Principal's\n// hygiene; it just cannot veto live work (the dispatch-surface twin of the\n// fold's satisfaction/resolution era-bounding).\nfunc (state *State) Blocks(escalation *EscalationState) bool {\n\tif escalation == nil || escalation.Resolved {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif escalation.RequestRef == 0 {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\trequest := state.Requests[escalation.RequestRef]\n\treturn request == nil || (!request.Canceled && !request.Satisfied)\n}\n\n// PlanState is one folded plan occurrence. State.Plans projects the latest\n// occurrence per request; plansBySeq retains exact historical occurrences.\ntype PlanState struct {\n\tSeq              uint64\n\tRequestRef       uint64\n\tSchemaVersion    uint16\n\tWrittenAt        string\n\tContentHash      string\n\tBasisLedgerSeq   uint64\n\tBasisHeadsDigest string\n\tUnmet            []string\n\tPlannedRuns      []PlannedRun\n\tInvalidReason    string\n\t// LoweringContext is the exact declared lowering-rule-set context the plan\n\t// was lowered under, folded verbatim from the plan_record. Zero (Digest\n\t// == \"\") for a rule-free plan, including every plan written before lowering\n\t// rules existed.\n\tLoweringContext LoweringRuleContext\n}\n\n// Fold rebuilds driver state from decoded ledger records.\nfunc Fold(all []records.DecodedRecord) *State {\n\tstate := NewState()\n\tstate.Apply(all)\n\treturn state\n}\n\n// NewState returns an empty fold state.\nfunc NewState() *State {\n\treturn &State{\n\t\tRequests:            map[uint64]*RequestState{},\n\t\tRuns:                map[uint64]*RunState{},\n\t\tIdentities:          map[string]*IdentityState{},\n\t\tGateResults:         map[gateKey][]GateResultState{},\n\t\tgateOperations:      map[string]gateResultOperationState{},\n\t\tEscalations:         map[uint64]*EscalationState{},\n\t\tImportedSubmissions: map[string]bool{},\n\t\tDrained:             map[string]bool{},\n\t\tSubmissions:         map[string]SubmissionState{},\n\t\tMarkedWaves:         map[uint64]bool{},\n\t\tHeadMovementWaves:   map[uint64]HeadMovementWaveState{},\n\t\tStalenessChunks:     map[uint64]map[int]bool{},\n\t\tPlans:               map[uint64]*PlanState{},\n\t\tplansBySeq:          map[uint64]*PlanState{},\n\t\tSchedulingDecisions: map[uint64][]SchedulingDecisionState{},\n\t\tFlakes:              map[string]bool{},\n\t\tWinners:             map[string]uint64{},\n\t\tRevocations:         map[string][]RevocationState{},\n\t\tResolutions:         map[uint64]*ResolutionState{},\n\t}\n}\n\n// Apply folds records appended after those already applied. Folding a ledger\n// incrementally \u2014 any prefix, then any suffix \u2014 is equivalent to folding it\n// whole: Apply is the single per-record loop Fold itself runs, and the\n// fold-equivalence guard holds that invariant against drift.\nfunc (state *State) Apply(all []records.DecodedRecord) {\n\tfor _, record := range all {\n\t\tstate.LastSeq = record.Seq\n\t\tpayload := record.Payload\n\t\tswitch record.Type {\n\t\tcase \"compilation_request\":\n\t\t\tsubject, _ := payload[\"subject\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\ttargetMap, _ := payload[\"target\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\tissuer, _ := payload[\"issuer\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\trequest := &RequestState{\n\t\t\t\tSeq:           record.Seq,\n\t\t\t\tSchemaVersion: record.SchemaVersion,\n\t\t\t\tSubject:       str(subject, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\t\tPrincipal:     str(issuer, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\t\tNote:          str(payload, \"note\"),\n\t\t\t\tTargetID:      str(targetMap, \"state_id\"),\n\t\t\t\tTargetVer:     intOf(targetMap, \"predicate_version\"),\n\t\t\t\tDelegationRef: str(issuer, \"delegation_ref\"),\n\t\t\t\tIssuerKind:    str(issuer, \"kind\"),\n\t\t\t\tDormant:       str(payload, \"replacement_mode\") == \"dormant\",\n\t\t\t\tSupersedes:    num(payload, \"supersedes\"),\n\t\t\t\tWrittenAt:     record.WrittenAt,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion >= 2 {\n\t\t\t\trequest.Payload = clonePayload(payload)\n\t\t\t\tprojection, err := targetmodel.ProjectionFromMap(targetMap)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t// A pinned projection that fails the strict decode folds in\n\t\t\t\t\t// the same degraded-but-linked shape as a v1 request: the\n\t\t\t\t\t// request stays visible with its recorded seq/subject/target\n\t\t\t\t\t// identity and keeps its authority linkage, but it never\n\t\t\t\t\t// plans. Strictness binds at write time (IssueRequest); the\n\t\t\t\t\t// fold never re-parses a broken pin into an executable\n\t\t\t\t\t// projection.\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.InvalidReason = err.Error()\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.Legacy = &LegacyRequestState{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tSchemaVersion: record.SchemaVersion,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tPayload:       clonePayload(payload),\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.TargetProjection = &projection\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.TargetID = projection.StateID\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.TargetVer = projection.PredicateVersion\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.ProgressOnly = projection.IsProgressOnly()\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trequest.Legacy = &LegacyRequestState{\n\t\t\t\t\tSchemaVersion: record.SchemaVersion,\n\t\t\t\t\tPayload:       clonePayload(payload),\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstate.Requests[record.Seq] = request\n\t\tcase \"cancellation_record\":\n\t\t\trequestRef := num(payload, \"request_ref\")\n\t\t\trequest := state.Requests[requestRef]\n\t\t\tif request == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion < 2 {\n\t\t\t\t// Legacy cancellation remains authoritative only over a legacy\n\t\t\t\t// request. It cannot acquire the exact target context needed to\n\t\t\t\t// terminate a v2 request or activate a replacement.\n\t\t\t\tif request.SchemaVersion < 2 {\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.Canceled = true\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.CancellationRef = record.Seq\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\texact, _ := payload[\"request\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\tissuer, _ := payload[\"issuer\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\tcause, _ := payload[\"cause\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\teffect, _ := payload[\"effect\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\toperationKey := str(payload, \"operation_key\")\n\t\t\tcomputedKey, _ := store.RunManifestHash(map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\"operation\": \"cancel_request\", \"request\": exact, \"issuer\": issuer,\n\t\t\t\t\"cause\": cause, \"effect\": effect, \"reason\": str(payload, \"reason\"),\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t// The exact cancellation names the request's semantic hash. A\n\t\t\t// degraded v2 request keeps its recorded hash string, so its\n\t\t\t// cancellation linkage never depends on the projection decoding.\n\t\t\twantHash := request.recordedTargetSemanticHash()\n\t\t\tif request.TargetProjection != nil {\n\t\t\t\twantHash = request.TargetProjection.SemanticHash\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif request.SchemaVersion != 2 || wantHash == \"\" ||\n\t\t\t\trequest.Canceled ||\n\t\t\t\tnum(exact, \"seq\") != requestRef || num(exact, \"schema_version\") != 2 || str(exact, \"subject\") != request.Subject ||\n\t\t\t\tstr(exact, \"target_semantic_hash\") != wantHash ||\n\t\t\t\tstr(issuer, \"kind\") != \"principal\" || str(issuer, \"identity\") == \"\" || operationKey == \"\" || operationKey != computedKey {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tswitch str(cause, \"kind\") {\n\t\t\tcase \"direct\":\n\t\t\t\tif len(effect) != 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase \"resolution\":\n\t\t\t\tresolution := state.Resolutions[num(cause, \"resolution_ref\")]\n\t\t\t\tif resolution == nil || resolution.RequestRef != requestRef ||\n\t\t\t\t\tresolution.EffectOperationKey == \"\" || resolution.EffectOperationKey != str(cause, \"effect_operation_key\") ||\n\t\t\t\t\tresolution.Resolver != str(issuer, \"identity\") {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treplacementRef := num(effect, \"activated_replacement_ref\")\n\t\t\t\tif resolution.Disposition == \"reissue\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tif replacementRef == 0 || replacementRef != resolution.ReplacementRequestRef {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\treplacement := state.Requests[replacementRef]\n\t\t\t\t\tif replacement == nil || !replacement.Dormant || replacement.Supersedes != requestRef || replacement.ActivatedBy != 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\treplacement.ActivatedBy = resolution.Seq\n\t\t\t\t} else if resolution.Disposition != \"cancel_request\" || replacementRef != 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tresolution.Effective = true\n\t\t\t\tif escalation := state.Escalations[resolution.EscalationRef]; escalation != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tescalation.Resolved = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trequest.Canceled = true\n\t\t\trequest.CancellationRef = record.Seq\n\t\tcase \"satisfaction_record\":\n\t\t\tif request, ok := state.Requests[num(payload, \"request_ref\")]; ok {\n\t\t\t\ttarget, _ := payload[\"target\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tsatisfied := false\n\t\t\t\tswitch {\n\t\t\t\tcase record.SchemaVersion >= 2 && request.SchemaVersion >= 2 && request.TargetProjection != nil:\n\t\t\t\t\tsatisfied = str(target, \"semantic_hash\") == request.TargetProjection.SemanticHash &&\n\t\t\t\t\t\tstr(payload, \"witness_digest\") != \"\"\n\t\t\t\tcase record.SchemaVersion >= 2 && request.SchemaVersion >= 2:\n\t\t\t\t\t// Satisfaction linkage never depends on the projection\n\t\t\t\t\t// decoding: a degraded v2 request still receives its exact\n\t\t\t\t\t// satisfaction when the recorded hash strings agree.\n\t\t\t\t\trecorded := request.recordedTargetSemanticHash()\n\t\t\t\t\tsatisfied = recorded != \"\" && str(target, \"semantic_hash\") == recorded &&\n\t\t\t\t\t\tstr(payload, \"witness_digest\") != \"\"\n\t\t\t\tcase record.SchemaVersion < 2 && request.SchemaVersion < 2:\n\t\t\t\t\t// A pre-v2 satisfaction keeps the meaning the older loader\n\t\t\t\t\t// recorded for it, bounded to its own era: it binds by\n\t\t\t\t\t// request_ref alone and can never satisfy an exact v2\n\t\t\t\t\t// request whose semantic hash it never named.\n\t\t\t\t\tsatisfied = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif satisfied {\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.Satisfied = true\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.SatisfactionRef = record.Seq\n\t\t\t\t\trequest.SatisfactionLevel = str(payload, \"claim_level\")\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"plan_record\":\n\t\t\trequestRef := num(payload, \"request_ref\")\n\t\t\tplan := &PlanState{\n\t\t\t\tSeq: record.Seq, RequestRef: requestRef,\n\t\t\t\tSchemaVersion: record.SchemaVersion, WrittenAt: record.WrittenAt,\n\t\t\t\tContentHash: str(payload, \"content_hash\"),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tplan.Unmet = stringsOf(payload, \"unmet\")\n\t\t\tplan.LoweringContext = loweringRuleContextFromValue(payload[\"lowering_context\"])\n\t\t\tif basis, ok := payload[\"basis\"].(map[string]any); ok {\n\t\t\t\tplan.BasisLedgerSeq = num(basis, \"ledger_seq\")\n\t\t\t\tplan.BasisHeadsDigest = str(basis, \"heads_digest\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion >= 2 {\n\t\t\t\tplanned, err := plannedRunsFromPayload(payload)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tplan.InvalidReason = err.Error()\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tplan.PlannedRuns = planned\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstate.Plans[requestRef] = plan\n\t\t\tstate.plansBySeq[record.Seq] = plan\n\t\tcase \"pass_run_opened\":\n\t\t\trun := &RunState{\n\t\t\t\tSeq:           record.Seq,\n\t\t\t\tSchemaVersion: record.SchemaVersion,\n\t\t\t\tPassID:        str(payload, \"pass_id\"),\n\t\t\t\tRequestRef:    num(payload, \"request_ref\"),\n\t\t\t\tPlanRef:       num(payload, \"plan_ref\"),\n\t\t\t\tPlanStepHash:  str(payload, \"plan_step_hash\"),\n\t\t\t\tDeadlineClass: str(payload, \"deadline_class\"),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trun.LoweringContext = loweringRuleContextFromValue(payload[\"lowering_context\"])\n\t\t\tif manifest, ok := payload[\"manifest\"].(map[string]any); ok {\n\t\t\t\trun.Manifest = manifest\n\t\t\t\trun.StepID = str(manifest, \"step_id\")\n\t\t\t\trun.Subject = str(manifest, \"subject\")\n\t\t\t\tif hash, err := store.RunManifestHash(manifest); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\trun.ManifestHash = hash\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif at, err := time.Parse(\"2006-01-02T15:04:05.000Z\", record.WrittenAt); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\trun.OpenedAt = at\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstate.Runs[record.Seq] = run\n\t\t\tif run.ManifestHash != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tif state.runsByManifestHash == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tstate.runsByManifestHash = map[string][]*RunState{}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tstate.runsByManifestHash[run.ManifestHash] = append(state.runsByManifestHash[run.ManifestHash], run)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"pass_run_closed\":\n\t\t\tif run, ok := state.Runs[num(payload, \"run_ref\")]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tif run.Open() && len(run.LaneBindings) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tstate.LastCapacityReleaseSeq = record.Seq\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\trun.Closures = append(run.Closures, str(payload, \"outcome\"))\n\t\t\t\trun.ClosureSources = append(run.ClosureSources, str(payload, \"closure_source\"))\n\t\t\t\trun.ClosureRefusalCodes = append(run.ClosureRefusalCodes, str(payload, \"refusal_code\"))\n\t\t\t\trun.ClosureDetails = append(run.ClosureDetails, closureDetail(payload))\n\t\t\t\tif str(payload, \"closure_source\") == \"scheduling_deferral\" {\n\t\t\t\t\trun.SchedulingDeferralSeq = record.Seq\n\t\t\t\t\tif retryAt, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, str(payload, \"retry_at\")); err == nil && retryAt.After(run.RetryAt) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\trun.RetryAt = retryAt\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif str(payload, \"closure_source\") == \"submission_refusal_v2\" {\n\t\t\t\tstate.Drained[str(payload, \"dispatch_id\")] = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"admission_decision\":\n\t\t\tstate.foldAdmissionDecision(payload)\n\t\tcase \"scheduling_decision\":\n\t\t\tcausedRun := uint64(0)\n\t\t\tif len(record.Causes) == 1 {\n\t\t\t\tcausedRun = \"run_ref\"),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion >= 2 {\n\t\t\t\tinput, _ := payload[\"input_preimage\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tready, _ := input[\"ready_set\"].([]any)\n\t\t\t\tif len(ready) != 1 {\n\t\t\t\t\tdecision.InvalidReason = \"v2 scheduling decision must name exactly one ready run\"\n\t\t\t\t\tdecision.RunRef = causedRun\n\t\t\t\t} else if run, ok := ready[0].(map[string]any); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tdecision.RunRef = num(run, \"run_ref\")\n\t\t\t\t\tif causedRun != 0 && decision.RunRef != causedRun {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tdecision.InvalidReason = \"v2 scheduling decision cause and ready run differ\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\tdecision.RunRef = causedRun\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tdecision.InvalidReason = \"v2 scheduling decision ready run is malformed\"\n\t\t\t\t\tdecision.RunRef = {\n\t\t\t\tstate.SchedulingDecisions[decision.RunRef] = append(state.SchedulingDecisions[decision.RunRef], decision)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"lane_binding\":\n\t\t\tif run, ok := state.Runs[num(payload, \"run_ref\")]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tbinding, err := scheduler.LaneBindingPayloadV1FromMap(payload)\n\t\t\t\tfolded := LaneBindingState{Seq: record.Seq, Payload: clonePayload(payload), Binding: binding}\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tfolded.InvalidReason = err.Error()\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\trun.BackendID = binding.BackendID\n\t\t\t\t\trun.DispatchID = binding.IndependencePartition\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\trun.LaneBindings = append(run.LaneBindings, folded)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"drive_session_record\":\n\t\t\tstate.LastDriveSession = DriveSessionState{\n\t\t\t\tSeq:         record.Seq,\n\t\t\t\tWrittenAt:   record.WrittenAt,\n\t\t\t\tQuiescence:  str(payload, \"quiescence_reason\"),\n\t\t\t\tNextHorizon: str(payload, \"next_horizon\"),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"artifact_admitted\":\n\t\t\tstate.foldArtifact(record)\n\t\tcase \"head_movement\":\n\t\t\tidentity := state.identity(str(payload, \"identity\"))\n\t\t\tmovement := str(payload, \"movement\")\n\t\t\teffect, _ := payload[\"effect\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\tlicensed, _ := payload[\"licensed_by\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\tfoldedMovement := HeadMovementState{\n\t\t\t\tSeq: record.Seq, SchemaVersion: record.SchemaVersion,\n\t\t\t\tIdentity: identity.ID, Movement: movement,\n\t\t\t\tFrom: num(payload, \"from_version\"), To: num(payload, \"to_version\"),\n\t\t\t\tOperationKey: str(payload, \"operation_key\"), Effect: clonePayload(effect),\n\t\t\t\tGateResults: anyToUints(licensed[\"gate_results\"]),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpriorHead := identity.Head\n\t\t\tstate.HeadMovements = append(state.HeadMovements, foldedMovement)\n\t\t\tif movement == \"retire\" {\n\t\t\t\tidentity.Head = 0\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tidentity.Head = num(payload, \"to_version\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif wave, ok := selectHeadMovementWave(state, record, foldedMovement, priorHead); ok {\n\t\t\t\tstate.HeadMovementWaves[record.Seq] = wave\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"gate_result\":\n\t\t\tsubject, _ := payload[\"subject\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\tkey := gateKey{\n\t\t\t\tIdentity: str(subject, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\t\tVersion:  num(subject, \"version_seq\"),\n\t\t\t\tGateID:   str(payload, \"gate_id\"),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdetail := str(payload, \"detail\")\n\t\t\tif detail == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t// Acceptance verdicts written by the accept/reject verbs\n\t\t\t\t// carry their adjudication text as \"reason\"; a rejection's\n\t\t\t\t// reason is the revision lane's only steer, so it must fold.\n\t\t\t\tdetail = str(payload, \"reason\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfolded := GateResultState{\n\t\t\t\tSeq:              record.Seq,\n\t\t\t\tSchemaVersion:    record.SchemaVersion,\n\t\t\t\tGateID:           key.GateID,\n\t\t\t\tClass:            str(payload, \"gate_class\"),\n\t\t\t\tPredicateVersion: intOf(payload, \"gate_predicate_version\"),\n\t\t\t\tOutcome:          str(payload, \"outcome\"),\n\t\t\t\tVerdict:          str(payload, \"verdict\"),\n\t\t\t\tDetail:           detail,\n\t\t\t\tEvidencePins:     gateEvidencePins(payload),\n\t\t\t\tOperationKey:     str(payload, \"operation_key\"),\n\t\t\t\tRequestRef:       num(payload, \"request_ref\"),\n\t\t\t\tTargetSemantic:   str(payload, \"target_semantic_hash\"),\n\t\t\t\tGateContractHash: str(payload, \"gate_contract_hash\"),\n\t\t\t\tPayload:          clonePayload(payload),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion == 2 {\n\t\t\t\toperationKey := str(payload, \"operation_key\")\n\t\t\t\tentry, exists := state.gateOperations[operationKey]\n\t\t\t\tpayloadHash, hashErr := store.RunManifestHash(payload)\n\t\t\t\tswitch {\n\t\t\t\tcase hashErr != nil:\n\t\t\t\t\tentry.conflict = true\n\t\t\t\tcase !exists:\n\t\t\t\t\tentry.payloadHash = payloadHash\n\t\t\t\tcase entry.payloadHash != payloadHash:\n\t\t\t\t\tentry.conflict = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tentry.latestSeq = record.Seq\n\t\t\t\tstate.gateOperations[operationKey] = entry\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif producing, ok := payload[\"producing_run\"].(map[string]any); ok {\n\t\t\t\tfolded.ProducingRunRef = num(producing, \"run_ref\")\n\t\t\t\tfolded.RunManifestHash = str(producing, \"run_manifest_hash\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion < 2 {\n\t\t\t\tfolded.InvalidReason = \"legacy gate result has no exact contextual force\"\n\t\t\t} else if err := validateFoldedGateResultV2(state, key, folded); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tfolded.InvalidReason = err.Error()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstate.GateResults[key] = append(state.GateResults[key], folded)\n\t\tcase \"staleness_event\":\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion < 2 {\n\t\t\t\tstate.MarkedWaves[num(payload, \"wave\")] = true\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\twave, _ := payload[\"wave\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tcause, _ := payload[\"cause\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tchunkKey, _ := stalenessChunkKey(num(cause, \"record_ref\"), str(wave, \"digest\"), intOf(wave, \"chunk_index\"), intOf(wave, \"chunk_count\"))\n\t\t\t\tif str(wave, \"operation_key\") == \"\" || str(wave, \"digest\") == \"\" || str(wave, \"chunk_key\") == \"\" ||\n\t\t\t\t\tintOf(wave, \"chunk_index\") < 0 || intOf(wave, \"chunk_count\") < 1 || str(wave, \"chunk_key\") != chunkKey {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tapplicable := false\n\t\t\t\tswitch str(cause, \"kind\") {\n\t\t\t\tcase \"decision_revoked\":\n\t\t\t\t\tapplicable = state.revocationChunkApplicable(record.Seq, payload)\n\t\t\t\tcase \"head_moved\":\n\t\t\t\t\tapplicable = map[int]bool{}\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tstate.StalenessChunks[causeRef][intOf(wave, \"chunk_index\")] = true\n\t\t\t\t\tif selected, ok := state.HeadMovementWaves[causeRef]; ok && len(state.StalenessChunks[causeRef]) == selected.ChunkCount {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tstate.MarkedWaves[causeRef] = true\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsubjects, _ := payload[\"subjects\"].([]any)\n\t\t\tfor _, entry := range subjects {\n\t\t\t\tsubject, _ := entry.(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tpin, _ := subject[\"pin\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tidentity := state.identity(str(pin, \"identity\"))\n\t\t\t\t// D0003 fixes Product lifecycle as supersession, never\n\t\t\t\t// staleness. Ignore historical waves that projected ordinary\n\t\t\t\t// reverse-pin invalidation onto Product Artifacts; replay must\n\t\t\t\t// recover the architectural invariant without rewriting the\n\t\t\t\t// immutable event.\n\t\t\t\tif identity.Role == \"product\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif version, ok := identity.Versions[num(pin, \"version_seq\")]; ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tversion.Stale = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"escalation\":\n\t\t\trequestRef := num(payload, \"request_ref\")\n\t\t\trunRef := num(payload, \"run_ref\")\n\t\t\tif requestRef == 0 {\n\t\t\t\tfor _, cause := range record.Causes {\n\t\t\t\t\tif state.Requests[cause] != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\trequestRef = cause\n\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif run := state.Runs[cause]; Its force\n\t\t\t\t// stays bounded to its own era \u2014 an escalation bound to an\n\t\t\t\t// exact v2 request still demands the exact v2 effect context.\n\t\t\t\tescalation.Disposition = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\texact, _ := payload[\"escalation\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\tresolver, _ := payload[\"resolver\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\teffectKey, _ := \"effect_operation_key\": effectKey,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tif num(exact, \"seq\") != escalationRef || str(exact, \"payload_hash\") != escalation.PayloadHash ||\n\t\t\t\tstr(exact, \"reason\") != ||\n\t\t\t\tstr(resolver, \"kind\") != \"principal\" || str(resolver, \"identity\") == \"\" || strings.TrimSpace(str(payload, \"note\")) == \"\" ||\n\t\t\t\tstr(payload, \"effect_operation_key\") != &&\n\t\t\t\t\tstr(payload, \"operation_key\") != \"\" && str(consequence, \"operation_key\") != \"\" && str(consequence, \"wave_digest\") != \"\" &&\n\t\t\t\t\tintOf(consequence, \"chunk_size\") > 0 \"chunk_count\"), Consequence: clonePayload(consequence),\n\t\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} else if decisionID != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tstate.Revocations[decisionID] = append(state.Revocations[decisionID], RevocationState{Seq: record.Seq, DecisionID: decisionID})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"submission_received\", \"submission_refused\":\n\t\t\tdispatchID := str(payload, \"dispatch_id\")\n\t\t\tif {\n\t\t\t\tstate.Submissions[dispatchID] = SubmissionState{\n\t\t\t\t\tDispatchID: dispatchID,\n\t\t\t\t\tRunRef:     num(payload, \"run_ref\"),\n\t\t\t\t\tStatus:     str(payload, {\n\t\t\t\tstate.IntegrationConflicts = append(state.IntegrationConflicts, foldIntegrationConflict(state, record))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"check_flake\":\n\t\t\t// A standing contradiction blocks Verified promotion (RFC 0010 \u00a75.6).\n\t\t\tstate.Flakes[str(payload, \"check_id\")+\"\\x00\"+str(payload, \"result_tree_hash\")] = true\n\t\tcase \"application_record\":\n\t\t\tapplication := \"change_set_hash\"),\n\t\t\t\tLinkedTreeHash: str(payload, \"linked_tree_hash\"),\n\t\t\t\tProductID:      str(payload, \"product_identity\"),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif record.SchemaVersion >= 2 {\n\t\t\t\tchange, _ := payload[\"change_set\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tproduct, _ := payload[\"product\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tbase, _ := payload[\"base\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tpacket, _ := payload[\"packet\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tmovement, _ := payload[\"movement\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\tlicensed, _ := payload[\"licensed_by\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\t\t\ttarget, _ := \"operation_key\")\n\t\t\t\tapplication.ChangeSet = foldedPin(change)\n\t\t\t\tapplication.Product = foldedPin(product)\n\t\t\t\tapplication.Base = foldedPin(base)\n\t\t\t\tapplication.Packet = \"semantic_hash\")\n\t\t\t\tapplication.TargetProjectionHash = str(target, \"projection_hash\")\n\t\t\t\tapplication.TargetProjectionObjectHash = str(projectionObject, \"content_hash\")\n\t\t\t\tapplication.TargetProjectionObjectAddress = str(projectionObject, \"address\")\n\t\t\t\tapplication.TargetProjectionObjectSize = num(projectionObject, \"size\")\n\t\t\t\tapplication.TargetDeclaration = clonePayload(declaration)\n\t\t\t\tapplication.TargetProjection = clonePayload(projection)\n\t\t\t\tif err := application)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (s *State) validateSchedulingDecision(decision SchedulingDecisionState) string {\n\tinput, ok := decision.Payload[\"input_preimage\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok || num(input, \"schema_version\") != 1 {\n\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision input preimage is malformed\"\n\t}\n\tevaluationID, err := store.RunManifestHash(input)\n\tif err != nil || str(decision.Payload, \"evaluation_id\") != evaluationID {\n\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision evaluation id does not match its input preimage\"\n\t}\n\tif str(decision.Payload, \"as_of\") == \"\" || str(decision.Payload, \"as_of\") != str(input, \"as_of\") {\n\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision as_of differs from its input preimage\"\n\t}\n\treadyValues, _ := input[\"ready_set\"].([]any)\n\tready, _ := readyValues[0].(map[string]any)\n\trun := s.Runs[decision.RunRef]\n\tif run == nil || !run.Open() || run.SchemaVersion < 2 ||\n\t\tstr(ready, \"run_manifest_hash\") != run.ManifestHash || str(ready, \"pass_id\") != run.PassID ||\n\t\tnum(ready, \"dispatch_version\") < 2 {\n\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision ready run differs from the opened run\"\n\t}\n\tselected, outcome, err := decisionBindingPreimage(decision)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err.Error()\n\t}\n\toutput, ok := decision.Payload[\"output_preimage\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok || num(output, \"schema_version\") != 1 {\n\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision output preimage is malformed\"\n\t}\n\tbindings, _ := output[\"bindings\"].([]any)\n\tif outcome != \"binding\" {\n\t\tif len(bindings) != 0 {\n\t\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision has a binding trace for an unbound outcome\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif outcome == \"deferral\" {\n\t\t\tretryAt := str(selected, \"retry_at\")\n\t\t\tif str(selected, \"reason\") != \"capacity_saturated\" || retryAt == \"\" || retryAt != str(output, \"next_horizon\") || retryAt != str(input, \"next_horizon\") {\n\t\t\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision deferral lacks its exact capacity reason or retry horizon\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tbinding, err := scheduler.LaneBindingPayloadV1FromMap(selected)\n\tif err != nil || binding.RunRef != decision.RunRef || binding.LaneID != str(ready, \"lane_id\") || binding.Attempt != int64(num(ready, \"attempt\")) {\n\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision binding differs from its ready run\"\n\t}\n\tif len(bindings) != 1 {\n\t\treturn \"v2 scheduling decision binding trace is absent or ambiguous\"\n\t}\n\ttrace, across graphs): it is fresh iff the\n// latest locally-admitted attestation for that foreign head reports the pinned\n// content hash. Historical occurrences carry no authenticated role\n\t\t// inheritance in the folded state, so they fail closed rather than\n\t\t// trusting a state-shaped predecessor chain.\n\t\treturn diagnostics\n\t}\n\trun := s.Runs[version.ProducedByRun]\n\tif run == nil || run.Manifest == nil {\n\t\treturn diagnostics\n\t}\n\tinputPins, _ := run.Manifest[\"input_pins\"].([]any)\n\tsemantic := map[store.Pin]int{}\n\tdeclaredDiagnostic := map[store.Pin]int{}\n\tfor _, raw := range inputPins {\n\t\tvalue, _ := raw.(map[string]any)\n\t\tpin := store.Pin{\n\t\t\tRepoID: str(value, \"repo_id\"), Identity: str(value, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\tVersionSeq: num(value, \"version_seq\"), ContentHash: str(value, \"content_hash\"),\n\t\t\tAnchor: str(value, \"anchor\"), ElementHash: str(value, \"element_hash\"),\n\t\t}\n\t\tif pin.Identity != \"\" && pin.ContentHash != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif str(value, \"role\") == \"diagnostic\" {\n\t\t\t\tdeclaredDiagnostic[pin]++\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tsemantic[pin]++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tprojected := map[store.Pin]int{}\n\tfor _, edge := range artifactVersionEdges(version) {\n\t\tif edge.Kind == \"derived_from\" {\n\t\t\tprojected[edge.Pin]++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tfor pin, declared := range declaredDiagnostic {\n\t\t// Before diagnostic-role projection was fixed, admission emitted S+D\n\t\t// identical derived_from occurrences; afterward it emits S. The edge\n\t\t// plane has no role bit, so only the excess above the sealed semantic\n\t\t// occurrence count is authenticated diagnostic projection.\n\t\texcess := projected[pin] - semantic[pin]\n\t\tif excess > declared {\n\t\t\texcess = declared\n\t\t}\n\t\tif excess > 0 {\n\t\t\tdiagnostics[pin] = excess\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn diagnostics\n}\n\n// intrinsicDiagnosticArtifactPin reports provenance context identified by the\n// graph itself, independently of producer-manifest diagnostic roles.\nfunc (s *State) intrinsicDiagnosticArtifactPin(identityID string, pin store.Pin) bool {\n\tif pin.Identity == identityID {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tif pinned, ok := s.Identities[pin.Identity]; ok && pinned.Role == \"evidence\" {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tif s.versionRefused(pin.Identity, pin.VersionSeq) {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// FreshnessEdges filters a version's edge set to the declared data and\n// constraint dependencies that participate in invalidation. Diagnostic pins\n// remain in provenance but confer no lifecycle force.\nfunc (s *State) FreshnessEdges(identityID string, version *VersionState) []store.Edge {\n\tif version == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tdiagnostics := s.diagnosticPinsForVersion(identityID, version)\n\tedges := append([]store.Edge(nil), version.Edges...)\n\tif len(edges) == 0 {\n\t\tfor _, pin := range version.DerivedFrom {\n\t\t\tedges = append(edges, store.Edge{Kind: \"derived_from\", Pin: pin})\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, pin := range version.ConstrainedBy {\n\t\t\tedges = append(edges, store.Edge{Kind: \"constrained_by\", Pin: pin})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tout := make([]store.Edge, 0, len(edges))\n\tfor _, edge := range edges {\n\t\tif edge.Kind != \"derived_from\" && edge.Kind != \"constrained_by\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\texplicitDiagnostic := edge.Kind == \"derived_from\" && diagnostics[edge.Pin] > 0\n\t\tif explicitDiagnostic {\n\t\t\tdiagnostics[edge.Pin]--\n\t\t}\n\t\tif explicitDiagnostic || s.intrinsicDiagnosticArtifactPin(identityID, edge.Pin) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tout = append(out, edge)\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// FreshnessPins is the pin-only projection used by claim and gate code.\nfunc (s *State) FreshnessPins(identityID string, version *VersionState) []store.Pin {\n\tedges := s.FreshnessEdges(identityID, version)\n\tout := make([]store.Pin, 0, len(edges))\n\tfor _, edge := range edges {\n\t\tout = append(out, edge.Pin)\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// versionRefused reports whether any gate's latest result refused the exact\n// pinned version.\nfunc (s *State) versionRefused(identity string, versionSeq uint64) bool {\n\tfor key, results := range s.GateResults {\n\t\tif key.Identity != identity || key.Version != versionSeq {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif latest := latestResult(results); latest != nil && latest.Outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc (s *State) LaneWon(run *RunState) bool {\n\tif run == nil || run.ManifestHash == \"\" || run.LaneID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t_, ok := s.Winners[winnerKey(run.ManifestHash, run.LaneID)]\n\treturn ok\n}\n\nfunc winnerKey(manifestHash, laneID string) string {\n\treturn manifestHash + \"\\x00\" + laneID\n}\n\nfunc pinsOf(container map[string]any, key string) []store.Pin {\n\tlist, _ := container[key].([]any)\n\tvar out []store.Pin\n\tfor _, entry := range list {\n\t\tpin, _ := entry.(map[string]any)\n\t\tout = append(out, store.Pin{\n\t\t\tRepoID:      str(pin, \"repo_id\"),\n\t\t\tIdentity:    str(pin, \"identity\"),\n\t\t\tVersionSeq:  num(pin, \"version_seq\"),\n\t\t\tContentHash: str(pin, \"content_hash\"),\n\t\t\tAnchor:      str(pin, \"anchor\"),\n\t\t\tElementHash: str(pin, \"element_hash\"),\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc str(payload map[string]any, key string) string {\n\tif payload == nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tvalue, _ := payload[key].(string)\n\treturn value\n}\n\nfunc closureDetail(payload map[string]any) string {\n\tif detail := str(payload, \"detail\"); detail != \"\" {\n\t\treturn detail\n\t}\n\treturn str(payload, \"closure_reason\")\n}\n\nfunc num(payload map[string]any, key string) uint64 {\n\tif payload == nil {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tvalue, ok := payload[key].(int64)\n\tif !ok || value < 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn uint64(value)\n}\n\nfunc intOf(payload map[string]any, key string) int {\n\treturn int(num(payload, key))\n}\n\nfunc clonePayload(input map[string]any) map[string]any {\n\tif input == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tout := make(map[string]any, len(input))\n\tfor key, value := range input {\n\t\tout[key] = clonePayloadValue(value)\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\nfunc clonePayloadValue(value any) any {\n\tswitch typed := value.(type) {\n\tcase map[string]any:\n\t\treturn clonePayload(typed)\n\tcase []any:\n\t\tout := make([]any, len(typed))\n\t\tfor i, item := range typed {\n\t\t\tout[i] = clonePayloadValue(item)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn out\n\tcase []byte:\n\t\treturn append([]byte(nil), typed...)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn typed\n\t}\n}\n",
