Unlike the\n// retired baseline-quality shortcut, this does not inspect or redefine\n// backend quality; it enforces the semantic evaluation tier itself.\nfunc (s *Session) tierZeroExcludedAliasingClasses() []string {\n\tseen := map[string]bool{}\n\tvar classes []string\n\tfor _, declaration := range s.Backends {\n\t\tclass := declaration.AliasingClass\n\t\tif class == \"\" || class == \"deterministic\" || seen[class] {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[class] = true\n\t\tclasses = append(classes, class)\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(classes)\n\treturn classes\n}\n\nfunc payloadMap(container map[string]any, key string) map[string]any {\n\tvalue, _ := container[key].(map[string]any)\n\treturn value\n}\n\nfunc latestCandidate(identity *IdentityState) *VersionState {\n\tfor i := len(identity.Order) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tversion := identity.Versions[identity.Order[i]]\n\t\tif version.Seq == identity.Head {\n\t\t\treturn nil // newest version already accepted\n\t\t}\n\t\tif version.ProductionMode == \"re_stamp\" {\n\t\t\treturn nil // re-stamps move heads through their license, not gates\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn version\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// deferCheckContractConflict reports whether err is a gate-contract semantic\n// environment conflict for this exact check-class gate on a candidate that\n// planning already owns a route for: the candidate carries a recorded check\n// refusal, so the revision ladder will produce a successor under the current\n// contract and stamping the old candidate is impossible by construction\n// (live 2026-07-19: the RFC 0010 amendment moved work-graph-legality and\n// every wake died in progress on the refused pre-amendment work-graph\n// candidate). Without a recorded refusal the conflict stays loud at the\n// call site unless the review search proves a regeneration route.\nfunc (s *Session) deferCheckContractConflict(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, err error, spec GateSpec, report *Report) bool {\n\tvar conflict *gateSemanticEnvironmentConflictError\n\tif !errors.As(err, &conflict) || conflict.kind != \"gate-contract\" || conflict.id != spec.ID || spec.Class != \"check\" {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif _, _, refused := s.checkRefused(state, identity, candidate); !refused {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tappendConflictNote(report, identity.ID, candidate.Seq, err)\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// appendConflictNote records the speaking exact-context regeneration note\n// once per session report.\nfunc appendConflictNote(report *Report, identityID string, candidateSeq uint64, err error) {\n\tif report == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tnote := fmt.Sprintf(\"candidate %s@%d requires exact-context regeneration: %v\", identityID, candidateSeq, err)\n\tfor _, existing := range report.Notes {\n\t\tif existing == note {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, note)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) progressCandidate(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, kind KindSpec, report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tactions := 0\n\tdeferSemanticEnvironmentConflict := func(err error, spec GateSpec) bool {\n\t\tif s.deferCheckContractConflict(state, identity, candidate, err, spec, report) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar conflict *gateSemanticEnvironmentConflictError\n\t\tif !errors.As(err, &conflict) || conflict.kind != \"gate-contract\" || conflict.id != spec.ID {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\t_, search, _ := s.candidateReviewPending(state, identity, candidate)\n\t\tif search.Regenerate == nil {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tappendConflictNote(report, identity.ID, candidate.Seq, err)\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tinputsFresh := true\n\tfor _, pin := range state.FreshnessPins(identity.ID, candidate) {\n\t\tif !state.PinFresh(pin) {\n\t\t\tinputsFresh = false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar licensing []uint64\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec, ok := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn actions, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: gate %q not cataloged\", gateID)\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: identity.ID, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\tresults := state.GateResults[key]\n\t\tlatest := latestResult(results)\n\t\tif latest != nil && s.gateResultApplicable(state, identity, candidate, spec, *latest) {\n\t\t\tif latest.Outcome == \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, latest.Seq)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// integration-checks is a CAS against reality (tree_moved and\n\t\t\t// kin): a refusal recorded under yesterday's reality must\n\t\t\t// re-evaluate as reality moves, appending only when the outcome\n\t\t\t// flips \u2014 a refused candidate under a since-repaired predicate\n\t\t\t// or a since-moved head is otherwise wedged forever behind the\n\t\t\t// winner rule. work-graph-legality shares the shape: its check\n\t\t\t// resolution reads the effective check registry, which is\n\t\t\t// registration state that moves outside the graph \u2014 a work-graph\n\t\t\t// refused before a repository declared its registry (the vitae\n\t\t\t// wedge, 2026-07-07: acceptance_check_unresolvable under a\n\t\t\t// then-absent registry, cured by the declaration and by nothing\n\t\t\t// a plan revision can see) must re-evaluate once the registry\n\t\t\t// resolves, or the identity is wedged behind a stale refusal.\n\t\t\tif (gateID == \"integration-checks\" || gateID == \"work-graph-legality\") && spec.Class == \"check\" {\n\t\t\t\tvar evidencePins []store.Pin\n\t\t\t\tif gateID == \"integration-checks\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tevidence := s.integrationLinkEvidence(state, candidate)\n\t\t\t\t\tif !evidence.Ready {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tevidencePins = evidence.Pins\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\toutcome, detail := s.evaluateCheckGate(state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\t\t\t\tif outcome == \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tseq, err := s.appendGateResultWithEvidencePins(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, \"\", evidencePins, inputsFresh, detail)\n\t\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, seq)\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn actions, nil // refused gate: candidate stays inert; planning owns revision\n\t\t}\n\t\tswitch spec.Class {\n\t\tcase \"check\":\n\t\t\tvar evidencePins []store.Pin\n\t\t\tif gateID == \"integration-checks\" {\n\t\t\t\tevidence := s.integrationLinkEvidence(state, candidate)\n\t\t\t\tif !evidence.Ready {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tevidencePins = evidence.Pins\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\toutcome, detail := s.evaluateCheckGate(state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\t\t\tseq, err := s.appendGateResultWithEvidencePins(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, \"\", evidencePins, inputsFresh, detail)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tif deferSemanticEnvironmentConflict(err, spec) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tif outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, seq)\n\t\tcase \"review\":\n\t\t\tsearch := s.findReviewEvidence(state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\t\t\tappendReviewDecline(report, identity, candidate, search.NewestDecline)\n\t\t\tappendReviewDecline(report, identity, candidate, search.Regenerate)\n\t\t\tappendReviewDecline(report, identity, candidate, search.Blocked)\n\t\t\tif search.Verdict == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil // review not yet performed: the plan carries the step\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\toutcome := \"pass\"\n\t\t\tif !gate.ReviewVerdictAccepting(search.Verdict) {\n\t\t\t\toutcome = \"fail\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tseq, err := s.appendGateResultWithEvidence(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, search.Verdict, search.Evidence, inputsFresh)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\tif outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlicensing = append(licensing, seq)\n\t\tcase \"acceptance\":\n\t\t\treturn actions, nil // closes only by Principal authority (accept verb)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif !inputsFresh {\n\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, \"head movement suspended (stale inputs): \"+identity.ID)\n\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t}\n\tif run, ok := state.Runs[candidate.ProducedByRun]; ok && run.PassID == \"integration\" && kind.Role == \"product\" {\n\t\trequest, err := s.integrationEffectRequest(state, identity, candidate, licensing)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := s.Graph.ApplyIntegrationEffectV2(request, s.Now); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn actions + 1, nil\n\t}\n\n\tappended, err := s.moveCandidateHeadV2(state, identity, candidate, kind, licensing)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, errHeadTransitionContextChanged) {\n\t\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, err.Error())\n\t\t\treturn actions, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn actions, err\n\t}\n\tif appended {\n\t\tactions++\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\nfunc latestResult(results []GateResultState) *GateResultState {\n\tif len(results) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn &results[len(results)-1]\n}\n\n// evaluateCheckGate dispatches the check-class gate predicates: all\n// execution-free Compiler Core computations over content (RFC 0010 \u00a71). The\n// default predicate is the v0 mechanical gate \u2014 durable body plus anchor\n// continuity within the policy retirement budget.\nfunc (s *Session) evaluateCheckGate(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, spec GateSpec) (string, string) {\n\tswitch spec.ID {\n\tcase \"receipt-checks\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateReceiptChecks(state, candidate)\n\tcase \"verification-report-checks\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateReportChecks(state, candidate)\n\tcase \"integration-checks\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateIntegrationChecks(state, identity, candidate)\n\tcase \"work-graph-legality\":\n\t\treturn s.evaluateWorkGraphLegality(state, identity, candidate)\n\tcase \"packet-checks\":\n\t\t// A change-set that pins a work-graph packet is a build-flow output and\n\t\t// faces the strict packet-checks predicate. A cleanly-parsed one that\n\t\t// pins none is the single-change-set grain (integrated@2, E4) and falls\n\t\t// through to the default mechanical gate \u2014 the build flow strengthens,\n\t\t// invalidates nothing. A body that is not a well-formed change-set fails\n\t\t// closed here; garbage never reaches the lenient default gate.\n\t\tswitch s.changeSetPacketDisposition(candidate) {\n\t\tcase \"strict\":\n\t\t\treturn s.evaluatePacketChecks(state, identity, candidate)\n\t\tcase \"fail\":\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"change-set body is not a well-formed change-set\"\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t// E4 grain drops the packet predicate, never the change-set's own\n\t\t\t// arithmetic. Every consumer downstream \u2014 the verifier's tree\n\t\t\t// composition, the integration linker, the code-tree receipt bind \u2014\n\t\t\t// applies this overlay to this base and demands exactly the declared\n\t\t\t// result tree; a body that fails its own arithmetic is doomed at all\n\t\t\t// three and can never be repaired by retrying. Admitting it buys a\n\t\t\t// permanently unverifiable head (BUG-2026-07-23: head 72075 declared\n\t\t\t// 827b44a1 while its 32-file overlay over the empty base yields\n\t\t\t// c8d6cf8f; the strict path had derived it, the lenient path did not,\n\t\t\t// and the funnel re-dispatched the doomed compose until its budgets\n\t\t\t// escalated). Deriving here is the same execution-free content\n\t\t\t// computation the strict predicate already performs.\n\t\t\tif outcome, detail := s.evaluateChangeSetAnchoredBase(state, identity, candidate); outcome != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn outcome, detail\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif outcome, detail := s.evaluateChangeSetResultTree(state, candidate); outcome != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn outcome, detail\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif candidate.BodyAddress == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"fail\", \"candidate has no durable body\"\n\t}\n\tif identity.Head != 0 {\n\t\tprior := identity.Versions[identity.Head]\n\t\tresult := gate.EvaluateAnchorContinuity(gate.AnchorContinuityInput{\n\t\t\tPrior:            prior.Anchors,\n\t\t\tCandidate:        candidate.Anchors,\n\t\t\tRetired:          toGateRetired(candidate.RetiredAnchors),\n\t\t\tRetirementBudget: s.Policy.RetirementBudget,\n\t\t})\n\t\tif result.Outcome == gate.AnchorContinuityRefuse {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"anchor continuity refused: missing \" + strings.Join(result.MissingAnchors, \",\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Outcome == gate.AnchorContinuityReviewRequired {\n\t\t\treturn \"fail\", \"anchor retirement budget exceeded: review required\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"pass\", \"\"\n}\n\nfunc toGateRetired(retired []store.RetiredAnchor) []gate.RetiredAnchor {\n\tout := make([]gate.RetiredAnchor, len(retired))\n\tfor i, entry := range retired {\n\t\tout[i] = gate.RetiredAnchor{Anchor: entry.Anchor, Reason: entry.Reason}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\ntype reviewEvidenceDecline struct {\n\tPin              store.Pin\n\tCode             string\n\tDetail           string\n\tCandidateContext bool\n}\n\ntype reviewEvidenceSearch struct {\n\tVerdict        string\n\tEvidence       map[string]any\n\tCandidateBound int\n\tNewestDecline  *reviewEvidenceDecline\n\tRegenerate     *reviewEvidenceDecline\n\tBlocked        *reviewEvidenceDecline\n}\n\ntype candidateReviewVersion struct {\n\tidentity string\n\tversion  *VersionState\n}\n\n// findReviewEvidence looks for admitted Review Ledgers that pin the exact\n// candidate. It returns both applicable evidence and the newest disqualified\n// candidate-bound ledger so a strict-context decline is visible and a new\n// review objective can be derived without releasing the Store's winner.\nfunc (s *Session) findReviewEvidence(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, spec GateSpec) reviewEvidenceSearch {\n\tresult := reviewEvidenceSearch{}\n\tcontext, err := exactGateCandidateContextFrom(s.getObject, s.Graph, s.Catalog, state, identity, candidate, spec)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tpin := store.Pin{Identity: identity.ID, VersionSeq: candidate.Seq, ContentHash: candidate.ContentHash}\n\t\tvar conflict *gateSemanticEnvironmentConflictError\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &conflict) {\n\t\t\tresult.Regenerate = \"candidate_context_blocked\", Detail: err.Error(), CandidateContext: true}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\tif !s.gateRunEnvironmentCurrent(state, context.run, spec) {\n\t\tresult.Regenerate = &reviewEvidenceDecline{\n\t\t\tPin:  store.Pin{Identity: identity.ID, VersionSeq: candidate.Seq, ContentHash: candidate.ContentHash},\n\t\t\tCode: \"producer_environment_stale\", Detail: \"candidate producer semantic environment is no longer current\", CandidateContext: true,\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\tvar candidates []candidateReviewVersion\n\tfor _, id := range sortedIdentityIDs(state) {\n\t\tevidenceIdentity := state.Identities[id]\n\t\tif evidenceIdentity.Kind != \"review-ledger\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, seq := range evidenceIdentity.Order {\n\t\t\tversion := version})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tsort.Slice(candidates, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\tif candidates[i].version.Seq == candidates[j].version.Seq {\n\t\t\treturn candidates[i].identity < candidates[j].identity\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn candidates[i].version.Seq > candidates[j].version.Seq\n\t})\n\tresult.CandidateBound = len(candidates)\n\tfor _, candidateReview := range candidates {\n\t\tversion := candidateReview.version\n\t\tpin := store.Pin{Identity: candidateReview.identity, VersionSeq: version.Seq, ContentHash: version.ContentHash}\n\t\tdecline := func(code, detail string) {\n\t\t\tif result.NewestDecline == nil && result.Regenerate == nil && result.Blocked == nil {\n\t\t\t\tresult.NewestDecline = &reviewEvidenceDecline{Pin: pin, Code: code, Detail: detail}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tblock := func(code, detail string) {\n\t\t\tif result.NewestDecline == nil && result.Regenerate == nil && result.Blocked == nil {\n\t\t\t\tresult.Blocked = &reviewEvidenceDecline{Pin: pin, Code: code, Detail: detail}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !gate.ReviewVerdictValid(version.ReviewVerdict) {\n\t\t\tdecline(\"review_verdict_invalid\", fmt.Sprintf(\"verdict %q is outside D0001.C6's closed vocabulary\", version.ReviewVerdict))\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tevidenceRun := state.Runs[version.ProducedByRun]\n\t\tif evidenceRun != nil {\n\t\t\tif strong primary floor; that review is additional-signal-only and cannot be the sole evidence for a review gate\")\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tpinValue := map[string]any{\"identity\": \"pass\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := buildGateResultPayloadV2From(s.getObject, s.Graph, s.Catalog, state, identity, candidate, spec, outcome, version.ReviewVerdict,\n\t\t\t[]store.Pin{pin}, allPinsFresh(state, identity.ID, candidate), nil, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\tvar evidenceErr *reviewEvidenceDeclinedError\n\t\t\tif errors.As(err, &evidenceErr) {\n\t\t\t\tif evidenceErr.Regenerate {\n\t\t\t\t\tproducerRun := state.Runs[candidate.ProducedByRun]\n\t\t\t\t\tevidenceRun := state.Runs[version.ProducedByRun]\n\t\t\t\t\tswitch {\n\t\t\t\t\tcase !s.gateRunEnvironmentCurrent(state, producerRun, spec):\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif result.NewestDecline == nil && result.Regenerate == nil && result.Blocked == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tresult.Regenerate = &reviewEvidenceDecline{Pin: pin, Code: evidenceErr.Detail)\n\t\t\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t\t\t\tblock(evidenceErr.Code, evidenceErr.Detail)\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t} else if evidenceErr.Retryable {\n\t\t\t\t\tdecline(evidenceErr.Code, evidenceErr.Detail)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tblock(evidenceErr.Code, evidenceErr.Detail)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tblock(\"gate_context_blocked\", environment is no longer current\")\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult.Verdict = version.ReviewVerdict\n\t\tresult.Evidence = pinValue\n\t\treturn result\n\t}\n\treturn result\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) gateRunEnvironmentCurrent(state *State, run *RunState, spec GateSpec) bool {\n\tif s == nil || s.Graph == nil || run == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tgateBody := s.Catalog.GateBodies[spec.ID]\n\tif len(gateBody) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tenvironment, err := gateSemanticEnvironmentFrom(s.getObject, state, hashBytes(gateBody), spec.ID, []*RunState{run})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn s.gateEnvironmentCurrent(map[string]any{\"semantic_environment\": environment})\n}\n\nfunc reviewStepID(identity string, candidateSeq uint64, candidateBoundReviews int) string {\n\tstepID := \"review/\" + identity + \"/\" + fmt.Sprint(candidateSeq)\n\tif candidateBoundReviews > 0 {\n\t\tstepID += \"-r\" + fmt.Sprint(candidateBoundReviews)\n\t}\n\treturn stepID\n}\n\nfunc reviewStepReason(base string, decline *reviewEvidenceDecline) string {\n\tif decline == nil {\n\t\treturn base\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s; prior evidence %s@%d declined (%s): %s\", base, decline.Pin.Identity, decline.Pin.VersionSeq, decline.Code, decline.Detail)\n}\n\nfunc appendReviewDecline(report *Report, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, decline %s@%d (%s): %s\",\n\t\t\tidentity.ID, candidate.Seq, decline.Code, decline.Detail))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, fmt.Sprintf(\"review evidence %s@%d declined for %s@%d (%s): %s\",\n\t\tdecline.Pin.Identity, decline.Pin.VersionSeq, identity.ID, candidate.Seq, decline.Code, decline.Detail))\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) appendGateResult(identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, spec GateSpec, outcome, verdict, detail string, inputsFresh bool) (uint64, error) {\n\treturn s.appendGateResultWithEvidence(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, verdict, nil, inputsFresh, detail)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) appendGateResultWithEvidence(identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState, spec GateSpec, outcome, verdict string, evidence map[string]any, inputsFresh bool, detail ...string) (uint64, error) {\n\tvar evidencePins []store.Pin\n\tif evidence != nil {\n\t\tevidencePins = append(evidencePins, foldedPin(evidence))\n\t}\n\tdetailValue := \"\"\n\tif len(detail) > 0 && detail[0] != \"\" {\n\t\tdetailValue = detail[0]\n\t}\n\treturn s.appendGateResultWithEvidencePins(identity, candidate, spec, outcome, verdict, evidencePins, inputsFresh, bool, detail string) (uint64, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\treturn appendGateResultV2FromState(s.Graph, s.Catalog, state, func(current []records.DecodedRecord) (*State, error) {\n\t\treturn s.fold()\n\t}, identity.ID, candidate.Seq, spec, outcome, verdict, evidencePins, inputsFresh, nil, detail, s.Instance, s.Now)\n}\n\n// --- mark: eager staleness waves ---\n\nfunc (s *Session) mark() (int, error) {\n\tif s.stalenessRecoveryClean {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\tactions, err := recoverHeadMovementStaleness(s.Graph, s.Now, s.Instance)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\ts.stalenessRecoveryClean = true\n\t}\n\treturn actions, err\n}\n\n// --- plan ---\n\ntype planStep struct {\n\tStepID  string\n\tPassID  string\n\tSubject string\n\t// SubjectVersion/SubjectHash pin a candidate that has not head-moved yet.\n\t// Link-time verification uses this to attest the staged Product candidate,\n\t// never whichever Product head happens to exist when the durable plan runs.\n\tSubjectVersion uint64\n\tSubjectHash    string\n\tInputs         []string\n\tRevalidate     bool\n\t// RevalidationGateIDs are the exact current subject-kind gate contracts a\n\t// C10 revalidation reviewer must receive. They are planning context, not a\n\t// second gate declaration; their bodies are pinned in the run environment.\n\tRevalidationGateIDs []string\n\t// RevalidationKind is the exact stale subject's artifact kind. Its catalog\n\t// body is pinned in the run environment so the Store can independently\n\t// derive the complete subject gate vector at the re-stamp boundary.\n\tRevalidationKind string\n\tReason           string\n\t// CheckDetail carries a refusing check gate's recorded detail into the\n\t// revision lane's objective (derived from folded gate results, so it is\n\t// deterministic for a given ledger and needs no plan serialization).\n\tCheckDetail string\n\t// RejectionDetail carries the Principal's acceptance-gate rejection\n\t// reason into the fresh revision's objective: without it the lane only\n\t// sees the (passing) review ledger and reproduces the rejected shape \u2014\n\t// the RQ-7999 write-scope revision proved it.\n\tRejectionDetail string\n\n\t// Verification steps carry the signal ordinal, the sandbox profile the\n\t// scheduler assigns for execution-independence distinctness, and the\n\t// resolved check identities.\n\tSignal   int\n\tProfile  string\n\tCheckIDs []string\n\t// CodeTree marks a verification step whose subject is verified against a\n\t// materialized filesystem tree (composed base + change-set diff, RFC 0010\n\t// \u00a75.2) rather than a single artifact body \u2014 set when the code check set is\n\t// selected for a change-set subject.\n\tCodeTree bool\n\t// ProductTree marks verification of an exact Product candidate's\n\t// materialized filesystem tree. It is distinct from CodeTree, whose\n\t// materialization is composed-base + Change Set arithmetic.\n\tProductTree bool\n\n\t// TargetCheckAuthorityBridge marks the exact subject-grain build that\n\t// rebinds an authority-deficient Product target to a newly-added Work Graph\n\t// packet. The packet supplies execution authority; packet-checks additionally\n\t// requires this build to be dependency-free and a no-op. The marker is\n\t// sealed planning context, never authority by itself.\n\tTargetCheckAuthorityBridge bool\n\t// TargetCheckAuthorityOriginGraph is the immutable Work Graph version that\n\t// predates the bridge packet. Packet admission rechecks that the selected\n\t// packet was absent there, so \"new\" is durable sealed context rather than a\n\t// transient planner observation.\n\tTargetCheckAuthorityOriginGraph string\n\n\t// ExcludedAliasing is the independence exclusion set for this step,\n\t// computed from recorded attribution: a judgment review never binds to\n\t// the subject producer's aliasing class, and never to the deterministic\n\t// class (tier-0 has no judgment to give).\n\tExcludedAliasing []string\n\t// MinimumQuality is a scheduler hard constraint for this exact lane. Any\n\t// review intended as the sole judgment for a review gate must be at least\n\t// strong: baseline review is additional-signal-only and can never license\n\t// a gate or the sole re-stamp signal.\n\tMinimumQuality string\n\n\t// DiagnosticIDs are declared diagnostic inputs (W10): prior candidates,\n\t// Review Ledgers, reports, or other exact evidence that explains why a\n\t// fresh production is required. The step reason says what the lane must\n\t// resolve; the presence of a diagnostic never invents a review refusal.\n\tDiagnosticIDs []string\n\t// RecoveryContext is the exact durable operation that selected a\n\t// mechanical recovery. It is provenance/planning context, never an\n\t// authority input: the lane still receives only declared artifact pins.\n\tRecoveryContext map[string]any\n\n\t// Packet is the work-graph packet id a build step implements; the base it\n\t// composes and its write scope are derived from the pinned work-graph.\n\tPacket string\n\n\t// Batch carries the ordered change-set identities of a batch integration\n\t// step (Work Graph index order) \u2014 the deterministic topological link of the\n\t// Verified frontier (D0010.C8). Empty for single-change integration.\n\tBatch []string\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) plan(report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tactions := 0\n\tfor _, requestSeq := range sortedRequestSeqs(state) {\n\t\trequest := state.Requests[requestSeq]\n\t\tif request.Satisfied || request.Canceled || !request.PlanningActive() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\ttarget, ok := targetSpecForRequest(request)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif redirect, blocked := latestInapplicableRedirect(state, request.Seq); blocked {\n\t\t\tif !hasOpenRedirectApplicabilityEscalation(state, request.Seq, redirect.Seq) {\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := s.append(\"escalation\", \"driver\", []uint64{request.Seq, redirect.Seq}, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\t\"reason\": \"missing_authority\", \"detail\": fmt.Sprintf(\"redirect resolution %d exact artifact pin is no longer applicable\", redirect.Seq),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"subjects\": []any{redirect.RedirectPin.Identity}, \"blocking_scope\": redirect.RedirectPin.Identity,\n\t\t\t\t\t\"request_ref\": int64(request.Seq),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"context\":     map[string]any{\"resolution_ref\": int64(redirect.Seq), \"artifact_pin\": pinMap(*redirect.RedirectPin)},\n\t\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tunmet, steps := s.computeSteps(state, request, target)\n\t\tif len(unmet) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcleared, err := s.clearExecutablePlan(state, request)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif cleared {\n\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif request.ProgressOnly {\n\t\t\t\tif hasProgressDossier(state, request) {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := s.append(\"escalation\", \"driver\", []uint64{request.Seq}, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\t\"reason\":         \"ambiguous_goal\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\"detail\":         progressDossierDetail(request),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"subjects\":       []any{request.Subject},\n\t\t\t\t\t\"blocking_scope\": request.Subject,\n\t\t\t\t\t\"request_ref\":    int64(request.Seq),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"context\": map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"target\": request.TargetProjection.ToMap(),\n\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tevaluation, err := s.evaluateRequestedTarget(state, request)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tappended, err := s.appendSatisfactionV2(request, evaluation)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif appended {\n\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif step, revocation, blocked := s.firstRevokedAuthority(state, steps); blocked {\n\t\t\tif !hasOpenRevocationEscalation(state, request.Seq, step.Subject, revocation.DecisionID) {\n\t\t\t\tcauses := []uint64{request.Seq}\n\t\t\t\tif revocation.Seq != 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tcauses = append(causes, revocation.Seq)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := s.append(\"escalation\", \"driver\", causes, map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\t\"reason\": \"missing_authority\", \"detail\": \"revoked decision \" + revocation.DecisionID,\n\t\t\t\t\t\"subjects\": []any{step.Subject}, \"blocking_scope\": step.Subject, \"request_ref\": int64(request.Seq),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"context\": map[string]any{\"decision\": map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"identity\": revocation.DecisionID, \"version_seq\": int64(revocation.VersionSeq), \"content_hash\": revocation.ContentHash,\n\t\t\t\t\t}},\n\t\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tactions++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar prepared []preparedRun\n\t\tfor _, step := range steps {\n\t\t\trun, ready, err := s.prepareStep(state, request, step, report)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif ready {\n\t\t\t\tprepared = append(prepared, run)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\thash, plannedRuns, err := planContent(unmet, prepared)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif planRecordIsRedundant(state, state.Plans[request.Seq], hash) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tplanPayload := map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\"request_ref\":  int64(request.Seq),\n\t\t\t\"content_hash\": hash,\n\t\t\t\"basis\": map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\"ledger_seq\":   int64(state.LastSeq),\n\t\t\t\t\"heads_digest\": headsDigest(state),\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\"unmet\":        toAnyStrings(unmet),\n\t\t\t\"planned_runs\": plannedRuns,\n\t\t\t\"policy_pins\": []any{map[string]any{\n\t\t\t\t\"policy_id\": \"driver\",\n\t\t\t\t\"version\":   int64(s.Policy.PolicyVersion),\n\t\t\t}},\n\t\t}\n\t\tif context, ok := s.loweringContextValue(); ok {\n\t\t\tplanPayload[\"lowering_context\"] = context\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := s.appendSchema(\"plan_record\", 2, \"driver\", []uint64{request.Seq}, planPayload); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tactions++\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\n// planRecordIsRedundant reports whether a freshly computed plan would say\n// nothing the latest plan record does not already say.\n//\n// Content equality alone is not enough. openPlannedRun's witness demands that\n// the plan's BasisHeadsDigest equal the current heads digest, so a plan\n// carrying dispatchable runs whose basis has moved is undispatchable until it\n// is restamped \u2014 and identical content over moved heads is exactly a restamp,\n// not a duplicate. Suppressing it is a permanent wedge: the plan can never\n// refresh, every session refuses with \"plan head basis is no longer intact\",\n// and no later head movement heals it, because each one only re-confirms the\n// stale digest (BUG-2026-07-23 defect 3: harvest plans 71928 and 72039 were\n// stranded this way by one unrelated observation ECR and stayed stranded).\n// Same content over the same heads is still a duplicate and stays suppressed;\n// a plan with no planned runs has no run-open witness to satisfy, so its basis\n// carries no dispatch force and needs no restamp.\nfunc planRecordIsRedundant(state *State, latest *PlanState, hash string) bool {\n\tif state == nil || latest == nil || latest.ContentHash != hash {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif planRunEligibilityChangedAfter(state, latest) {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn len(latest.PlannedRuns) == 0 || latest.BasisHeadsDigest == headsDigest(state)\n}\n\n// clearExecutablePlan records that a formerly-ready plan now contains no\n// dispatchable work. Without this exact successor, a progress-only or\n// evidence-awaiting request would keep dispatching an obsolete PlannedRun even\n// after its planning conjuncts became true.\nfunc (s *Session) clearExecutablePlan(state *State, request *RequestState) (bool, error) {\n\tlatest := state.Plans[request.Seq]\n\tif latest == nil || latest.SchemaVersion < 2 || len(latest.PlannedRuns) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\thash, plannedRuns, err := planContent([]string{}, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\tif latest.ContentHash == hash {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\tclearedPayload := map[string]any{\n\t\t\"request_ref\": int64(request.Seq), \"content_hash\": hash,\n\t\t\"basis\": map[string]any{\"ledger_seq\": int64(state.LastSeq), \"heads_digest\": headsDigest(state)},\n\t\t\"unmet\": []any{}, \"planned_runs\": plannedRuns,\n\t\t\"policy_pins\": []any{map[string]any{\"policy_id\": \"driver\", \"version\": int64(s.Policy.PolicyVersion)}},\n\t}\n\tif context, ok := s.loweringContextValue(); ok {\n\t\tclearedPayload[\"lowering_context\"] = context\n\t}\n\t_, err = s.appendSchema(\"plan_record\", 2, \"driver\", []uint64{request.Seq}, clearedPayload)\n\treturn err == nil, err\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) firstRevokedAuthority(state *State, steps []planStep) (planStep, RevocationState, bool) {\n\tfor _, step := range steps {\n\t\tpass, ok := s.Catalog.Passes[step.PassID]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, decisionID := range pass.EnvironmentDecisions {\n\t\t\thash := hashBytes(s.Environment.Decisions[decisionID])\n\t\t\tidentity := state.Identities[decisionID]\n\t\t\tif identity == nil || identity.Head == 0 || identity.Versions[identity.Head] == nil ||\n\t\t\t\tidentity.Versions[identity.Head].ContentHash != hash || !state.DecisionRevoked(decisionID, identity.Head, hash) {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tentries := state.Revocations[decisionID]\n\t\t\tfor i := len(entries) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\t\t\tif entries[i].ContentHash == \"\" || entries[i].ContentHash == hash {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn step, entries[i], true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn step, RevocationState{DecisionID: decisionID, ContentHash: hash}, true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn planStep{}, RevocationState{}, false\n}\n\nfunc latestInapplicableRedirect(state *State, requestRef uint64) (*ResolutionState, bool) {\n\tvar latest *ResolutionState\n\tfor _, resolution := range state.Resolutions {\n\t\tif resolution.Disposition != \"redirect\" || resolution.RequestRef != requestRef || resolution.RedirectPin == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif latest == nil || resolution.Seq > latest.Seq {\n\t\t\tlatest = resolution\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif latest == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tpin := latest.RedirectPin\n\tidentity := state.Identities[pin.Identity]\n\tif identity == nil || identity.Head != pin.VersionSeq {\n\t\treturn latest, true\n\t}\n\tversion := identity.Versions[pin.VersionSeq]\n\treturn latest, version == nil || version.Stale || version.ContentHash != pin.ContentHash\n}\n\nfunc hasOpenRedirectApplicabilityEscalation(state *State, requestRef, resolutionRef uint64) bool {\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif !escalation.Resolved && escalation.Reason == \"missing_authority\" && escalation.RequestRef == requestRef &&\n\t\t\tescalation.RelatedResolutionRef == resolutionRef {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc hasOpenRevocationEscalation(state *State, requestRef uint64, subject, decisionID string) bool {\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif !escalation.Resolved && escalation.Reason == \"missing_authority\" && escalation.RequestRef == requestRef &&\n\t\t\tescalation.BlockingScope == subject && strings.Contains(escalation.Detail, decisionID) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// computeSteps derives the unmet conjuncts and the plan steps for a request:\n// graph reachability over catalog consumes/produces data (RFC 0006).\nfunc (s *Session) computeSteps(state *State, request *RequestState, target TargetSpec) ([]string, []planStep) {\n\tvar unmet []string\n\tvar steps []planStep\n\tplanned := map[string]bool{}\n\n\t// ensure walks the consumes/produces graph. conjunctKind is set only for\n\t// the target's directly-demanded kinds (\"\" for transitive inputs):\n\t// evidence_admitted asks for an admitted evidence version,\n\t// verified_dual_signal demands the Dual-Signal Rule over the subject's\n\t// accepted head, and accepted_head over a source pass demands a head\n\t// produced under this request \u2014 a new capture command is a new\n\t// utterance, so the head that satisfied an earlier request does not\n\t// satisfy this one.\n\tvar ensure func(kind, conjunctKind string) bool\n\tensure = func(kind, conjunctKind string) bool {\n\t\tif planned[targetCheckAuthorityRecoveryPlanKey] {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t\tidentity := subjectIdentity(request.Subject, kind)\n\n\t\tif conjunctKind == \"verified_dual_signal\" {\n\t\t\thead := state.HeadVersion(identity)\n\t\t\tif s.planTargetCheckAuthorityRecovery(state, request, identity, head, planned, &steps) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// The subject head must exist and be fresh before verification\n\t\t\t// is meaningful; ensure it through the ordinary walk first.\n\t\t\tif !ensure(kind, \"\") {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\thead = state.HeadVersion(identity)\n\t\t\t// A conclusively-rejected change-set head revises, never\n\t\t\t// re-verifies: a deterministic mechanical reject (go vet/test)\n\t\t\t// re-runs to the same NO, re-minting the verify objective until\n\t\t\t// the redispatch bound escalates (cap-b's signal-2-r14 loop).\n\t\t\t// Reject-verdict\u2192revision (stalls-need-no-judgment@4), mirroring\n\t\t\t// the packet frontier's verificationRejected routing.\n\t\t\tif rev := s.planVerificationRejectRevision(state, identity, head, planned); rev != nil {\n\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *rev)\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// A head that fails its own arithmetic cannot compose, so it can\n\t\t\t// never be verified. Consulted before the signal demand, exactly as\n\t\t\t// the tree-moved rebuild is under integrated_head.\n\t\t\tif rebuild, underivable := s.planUnderivableChangeSetRebuild(state, identity, head, planned); underivable {\n\t\t\t\tif rebuild != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *rebuild)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif revision, recovered := s.planPacketContextRecovery(state, request, identity, head, planned); recovered {\n\t\t\t\tif revision != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *revision)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsatisfied := s.planDualSignalForTarget(state, request, identity, head, planned, &steps)\n\t\t\treturn satisfied\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif conjunctKind == \"built\" {\n\t\t\t// The work-graph build frontier: fan out one build per ready\n\t\t\t// packet, verify each change-set (D0010.C8/C9).\n\t\t\treturn s.planWorkGraphBuild(state, request, kind, planned, &steps, ensure)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif conjunctKind == \"integrated_batch\" {\n\t\t\t// The whole work-graph linked into product lineage as a batch.\n\t\t\treturn s.planWorkGraphIntegration(state, request, kind, planned, &steps, ensure)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif conjunctKind == \"integrated_head\" {\n\t\t\tproductID := productIdentity(request.Subject)\n\t\t\tif s.planTargetCheckAuthorityRecovery(state, request, productID, state.HeadVersion(productID), planned, &steps) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Integration consumes claim_min: Verified \u2014 the change-set head\n\t\t\t// must be accepted, fresh, and dual-signal verified before the\n\t\t\t// linker stages anything.\n\t\t\tif !ensure(kind, \"\") {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\thead := state.HeadVersion(identity)\n\t\t\t// An application-bound head satisfies the conjunct outright: its\n\t\t\t// own integration moved the composed base, so every later check\n\t\t\t// against that base (tree-moved, re-verification) is vacuous for\n\t\t\t// it \u2014 consulted first or they loop forever on a done head.\n\t\t\tif s.applicationBound(state, request, identity, head, productID) {\n\t\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// A conclusively-rejected head revises, never re-verifies \u2014 the same\n\t\t\t// rule the verified_dual_signal branch applies, and it must hold\n\t\t\t// here too. A target that demands integrated_head:change-set without\n\t\t\t// also demanding verified_dual_signal:change-set (the shape of every\n\t\t\t// product/product_tree evaluator, semantic-and-durable-closure@2\n\t\t\t// included) reaches the dual-signal demand only through this branch,\n\t\t\t// so without this consult a deterministic reject re-mints the signal\n\t\t\t// pair on every wake forever. Live 2026-07-23: change-set head 75013\n\t\t\t// rejected all four code checks in both signals and the planner\n\t\t\t// minted signal-{1,2}-r0 through -r5 \u2014 the same re-mint shape as the\n\t\t\t// cap-b signal-2-r14 loop that stalls-need-no-judgment@4 retired on\n\t\t\t// the other branch only.\n\t\t\tif rev := s.planVerificationRejectRevision(state, identity, head, planned); rev != nil {\n\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *rev)\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// D0010.C8: a typed integration apply_failure routes the exact\n\t\t\t// losing Change Set back through build. Retrying the unchanged\n\t\t\t// deterministic integration objective can only reproduce the same\n\t\t\t// conflict and exhaust its attempt budget.\n\t\t\tif revision, failed := s.planIntegrationApplyFailureRevision(state, request, identity, head, planned); failed {\n\t\t\t\tif revision != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *revision)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Clause 4 (stalls-need-no-judgment@3), consulted BEFORE the\n\t\t\t// dual-signal demand: an unintegrated head whose pinned base no\n\t\t\t// longer composes can neither re-verify nor lawfully re-integrate\n\t\t\t// \u2014 the lawful plan is a bounded rebase-style build revision\n\t\t\t// against the current composed base, and demanding fresh signals\n\t\t\t// on the doomed head first would starve the rebuild forever.\n\t\t\tif rebuild, moved := s.planTreeMovedRebuild(state, request, identity, head, planned); moved {\n\t\t\t\tif rebuild != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *rebuild)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Same clause-four shape one step earlier: a head that does not\n\t\t\t// derive its own result tree can neither re-verify nor integrate.\n\t\t\tif rebuild, underivable := s.planUnderivableChangeSetRebuild(state, identity, head, planned); underivable {\n\t\t\t\tif rebuild != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *rebuild)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// A packetless head with an exact packet-bound revision ancestor is\n\t\t\t// not an E4 packetless build. Restore its immutable packet element\n\t\t\t// before target checks or integration can consume it.\n\t\t\tif revision, recovered := s.planPacketContextRecovery(state, request, identity, head, planned); recovered {\n\t\t\t\tif revision != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, *revision)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !s.planDualSignalForTarget(state, request, identity, head, planned, &steps) {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif productState, ok := state.Identities[productID]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tif candidate := latestCandidate(productState); candidate != nil &&\n\t\t\t\t\t!s.integrationRefused(state, candidate, productID) &&\n\t\t\t\t\ts.integrationCandidateEnvironmentCurrent(state, candidate) {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.planIntegrationLinkVerification(state, productID, candidate, planned, &steps)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false // staged; progressing through integration-checks\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// A refused candidate (tree_moved and kin) re-stages below.\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstepID := \"integrate/\" + productID + \"/\" + head.ContentHash[:16]\n\t\t\tif planned[stepID] {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tplanned[stepID] = true\n\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, planStep{\n\t\t\t\tStepID:  stepID,\n\t\t\t\tPassID:  \"integration\",\n\t\t\t\tSubject: productID,\n\t\t\t\tInputs:  []string{identity},\n\t\t\t\tReason:  \"verified change set awaiting integration\",\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif conjunctKind == \"review_admitted\" {\n\t\t\t// The harvest review posture (RFC 0008): a second-opinion Review\n\t\t\t// Ledger over the named artifact's current accepted head,\n\t\t\t// produced under THIS request \u2014 a prior review never satisfies a\n\t\t\t// new harvest utterance.\n\t\t\tartifact := harvestArtifact(request.Subject)\n\t\t\tif artifact == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn false // not a harvest subject; unmet and unplannable\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\thead := state.HeadVersion(artifact)\n\t\t\tif head == nil || head.Stale {\n\t\t\t\treturn false // issuance should have refused; nothing to review\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif s.reviewAdmittedUnder(state, request, head) != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstepID := \"harvest-review/\" + artifact + \"/\" + fmt.Sprint(request.Seq)\n\t\t\tif planned[stepID] {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tplanned[stepID] = true\n\t\t\texcluded := []string{\"deterministic\"}\n\t\t\tif head.AliasingClass != \"\" && head.AliasingClass != \"deterministic\" {\n\t\t\t\texcluded = append(excluded, head.AliasingClass)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsteps = append(steps, planStep{\n\t\t\t\tStepID:           stepID,\n\t\t\t\tPassID:           \"review\",\n\t\t\t\tSubject:          artifact,\n\t\t\t\tReason:           \"harvest: second-opinion review (delegated issuance)\",\n\t\t\t\tExcludedAliasing: excluded,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif conjunctKind == \"evidence_admitted\" {\n\t\t\tif latestVersion(state, identity) != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif planned[identity] {\n\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tplanned[identity] = true\n\t\t\tif producer, ok := s.Catalog.ProducerOf(kind); candidate != nil {\n\t\t\t\tif !s.candidateRetired(state, identityState, candidate) {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.progressPendingCandidate(state, request, kind, identity, identityState, candidate, planned, &steps)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// Retired (Principal-rejected at acceptance): terminal for\n\t\t\t\t// that version \u2014 a fresh revision is the only way forward.\n\t\t\t\t// The step id must be round-salted: the plain produce\n\t\t\t\t// objective was won by the run that made the rejected\n\t\t\t\t// candidate, so an unsalted re-production is refused at\n\t\t\t\t// admission as a duplicate (the winner rule).\n\t\t\t\tretired = candidate\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif retired == nil && headRetired {\n\t\t\t// The withdrawn head is the rejected version; Only such a\n// subject's first subject-grain change-set anchors against the repository.\nfunc subjectBornsProduct(request *RequestState) bool {\n\tif request == nil || request.TargetProjection == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif request.TargetProjection.Evaluation.SubjectRole == \"product\" {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tfor _, conjunct := range request.TargetProjection.PlanningConjuncts {\n\t\tif conjunct.Kind == \"integrated_head\" || conjunct.Kind == \"integrated_batch\" {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// headSatisfiesRequest applies the source-pass freshness rule: a head of a\n// kind whose producer consumes no artifacts (the input is the Principal's\n// utterance) satisfies only the request whose runs produced it.\nfunc (s *Session) headSatisfiesRequest(state *State, request *RequestState, kind string, head *VersionState) bool {\n\tproducer, ok := s.Catalog.ProducerOf(kind)\n\tif !ok || len(producer.Consumes) > 0 {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\trun, ok := state.Runs[head.ProducedByRun]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif run.RequestRef == request.Seq {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\t// A source pass takes no artifact input: its whole objective is the\n\t// Principal's utterance, carried verbatim into the run manifest as the\n\t// note (objective), while the request seq is NOT in the manifest. So two\n\t// requests with the SAME note against the same source-pass identity\n\t// produce a byte-identical artifact under a colliding manifest \u2014 the head\n\t// one already produced is exactly what the other would produce, and\n\t// re-producing it is refused by the winner rule as superseded_attempt,\n\t// looping until bounds_exhausted (won-objectives-stay-won). Such a head\n\t// therefore satisfies this request: identical utterances share their\n\t// source artifact. A genuinely new utterance (different note) is a\n\t// distinct manifest that correctly re-produces and admits, so it is not\n\t// matched here \u2014 the \"new capture command is a new utterance\" rule holds\n\t// exactly where the utterance actually differs.\n\tif producing, ok := state.Requests[run.RequestRef]; ok && producing.Note == request.Note {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// latestVersion returns an identity's newest admitted version, if any.\nfunc latestVersion(state *State, identity string) *VersionState {\n\tidentityState, ok := state.Identities[identity]\n\tif !ok || len(identityState.Order) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn identityState.Versions[identityState.Order[len(identityState.Order)-1]]\n}\n\n// candidateRetired reports whether the Principal refused the candidate at an\n// acceptance gate: a rejected revision is terminal for that version \u2014 it\n// neither holds the demand side nor receives further progression; a fresh\n// revision (a new version) is the only way forward.\nfunc (s *Session) candidateRetired(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) bool {\n\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[candidate.Kind]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif spec.Class != \"acceptance\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: identity.ID, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\tif latest := latestResult(state.GateResults[key]); latest != nil && latest.Outcome != \"pass\" {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// failureClosure builds a pass_run_closed{error} payload carrying the C3\n// diagnosis structure (stalls-need-no-judgment@3): stage, a class resolving\n// in the governed refusal-classes catalog, detail, and causal refs. When the\n// catalog is absent or the class does not validate, the payload degrades to\n// the legacy shape with detail preserved \u2014 readable, never refused, never\n// silently classless when the vocabulary is present.\nfunc (s *Session) failureClosure(runRef uint64, stage, class, detail string, causal []uint64) map[string]any {\n\tpayload := map[string]any{\n\t\t\"run_ref\": int64(runRef),\n\t\t\"outcome\": \"error\",\n\t\t\"detail\":  detail,\n\t}\n\tif len(causal) == 0 {\n\t\t// The run being closed is itself the minimal causal citation.\n\t\tcausal = []uint64{runRef}\n\t}\n\tif failure := s.failureValue(stage, class, detail, causal); failure != nil {\n\t\tpayload[\"failure\"] = failure\n\t}\n\treturn payload\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) failureValue(stage, class, detail string, causal []uint64) map[string]any {\n\tif len(causal) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\trefs := make([]string, 0, len(causal))\n\tfor _, seq := range causal {\n\t\trefs = append(refs, fmt.Sprintf(\"seq:%d\", seq))\n\t}\n\tfailure := admission.Failure{Stage: stage, Class: class, DetailOrEvidence: detail, CausalRefs: refs}\n\tif admission.ValidateFailure(failure, s.FailureClasses, true) != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tanyRefs := make([]any, 0, len(refs))\n\tfor _, r := range refs {\n\t\tanyRefs = append(anyRefs, r)\n\t}\n\treturn map[string]any{\n\t\t\"stage\":              stage,\n\t\t\"class\":              class,\n\t\t\"detail_or_evidence\": detail,\n\t\t\"causal_refs\":        anyRefs,\n\t}\n}\n\n// acceptanceRejectionDetail returns the recorded reason of the acceptance\n// gate result that retired the candidate \u2014 the Principal's steer for the\n// fresh revision. Its v2 effect pins both occurrences;\n// a body-equal version, a malformed movement, or an ambiguous duplicate has no\n// recovery force.\nfunc exactAcceptedHeadPredecessor(state *State, identity string, current *VersionState) (*VersionState, bool) {\n\tif state == nil || current == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tidentityState := state.Identities[identity]\n\tif identityState == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tvar predecessor *VersionState\n\tfor _, movement := range state.HeadMovements {\n\t\tif movement.SchemaVersion != 2 || movement.Identity != identity || movement.To != current.Seq || movement.Movement != \"supersede\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tcandidate, candidateOK := movement.Effect[\"candidate\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\texpected, expectedOK := movement.Effect[\"expected_head\"].(map[string]any)\n\t\tprior := identityState.Versions[movement.From]\n\t\tif !candidateOK || !expectedOK || prior == nil || movement.From == 0 || movement.From >= movement.To ||\n\t\t\tstr(movement.Effect, \"transition\") != \"gated\" ||\n\t\t\tfoldedPin(candidate) != (store.Pin{Identity: identity, VersionSeq: current.Seq, ContentHash: current.ContentHash}) ||\n\t\t\tfoldedPin(expected) != (store.Pin{Identity: identity, VersionSeq: prior.Seq, ContentHash: prior.ContentHash}) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, false\n\t\t}\n\t\tif predecessor != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, false\n\t\t}\n\t\tpredecessor = prior\n\t}\n\treturn predecessor, predecessor != nil\n}\n\n// exactPacketLineageAnchor recovers packet grain only from immutable,\n// gated accepted-head lineage. Live 2026-07-24: a re-issue of\n\t// stalls-need-no-judgment-d restamped the intent, staling the whole IR\n\t// ladder; the design's pending candidate (made under the prior request) was\n\t// planned for context-differs regeneration over a stale proposal that\n\t// progressPendingCandidate returned before ever refreshing.\n\tif s.planStaleRegenerationInputs(state, request, kind, identity, planned, steps) {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif legacyEraCandidate(state, candidate) {\n\t\tif kind == \"change-set\" && identity == subjectIdentity(request.Subject, \"change-set\") &&\n\t\t\ts.planLegacyChangeSetWorkGraph(state, request, candidate, planned, steps) {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif step, ok := s.exactContextRegenerationStep(state, request, kind, identity, identityState, candidate,\n\t\t\t\"legacy-era candidate cannot receive exact gate context: regenerate under the active request\"); An implementation-only movement\n// does not stale admitted history, but it does make re-execution meaningful:\n// same inputs under a different implementation are not evidence that the\n// upstream judgment artifact is deficient.\nfunc candidateProducerImplementationCurrent(state *State, candidate *VersionState, producer PassSpec) bool {\n\tif state == nil || candidate == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\trun := state.Runs[candidate.ProducedByRun]\n\tif run == nil || run.SchemaVersion < 2 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tpinned, ok := run.Manifest[\"pass\"].(map[string]any)\n\treturn ok && str(pinned, \"id\") == producer.ID &&\n\t\tintOf(pinned, \"contract_version\") == producer.ContractVersion &&\n\t\tintOf(pinned, \"impl_version\") == producer.ImplVersion\n}\n\n// candidateProducerContinuityPreimageCurrent protects the other input to D1\n// packetization: the accepted Work Graph whose packet anchors continuity must\n// preserve. its recorded detail:\n// a mechanical refusal is re-production work for the producing pass.\nfunc (s *Session) checkRefused(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) (string, string, bool) {\n\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[candidate.Kind]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n\t}\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif == nil || latest.Outcome != \"fail\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn gateID, latest.Detail, true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", \"\", false\n}\n\n// reviewRefused reports whether the candidate's latest review-class gate\n// result refused with a needs_revision/reject verdict, returning the refusing\n// Review Ledger's identity for diagnostic pinning.\nfunc (s *Session) reviewRefused(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) (string, bool) {\n\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[candidate.Kind]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn \"\", false\n\t}\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif spec.Class != \"review\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: identity.ID, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\tlatest := latestResult(state.GateResults[key])\n\t\tif latest == nil || latest.Outcome == \"pass\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// W7 gate results bind the exact evidence versions they evaluated. A\n\t\t// scan by subject hash can alias an older Review Ledger when\n\t\t// body-identical candidates repeat across versions, so only the recorded\n\t\t// pins may supply revision diagnostics. An evidence-free or unresolvable\n\t\t// refusal remains a refusal, but cannot infer a Review Ledger that the\n\t\t// immutable gate result did not name.\n\t\tfor _, pin := range latest.EvidencePins {\n\t\t\tevidence := state.Identities[pin.Identity]\n\t\t\tif evidence == nil || evidence.Kind != \"review-ledger\" {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tversion := evidence.Versions[pin.VersionSeq]\n\t\t\tif version == nil || version.ContentHash != pin.ContentHash {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif version.ReviewSubjectIdentity == identity.ID && version.ReviewSubjectHash == candidate.ContentHash && version.ReviewSubjectVersion == candidate.Seq {\n\t\t\t\treturn pin.Identity, true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", true\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", false\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) candidateAwaitsReview(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) bool {\n\t_, _, awaiting := s.candidateReviewPending(state, identity, candidate)\n\treturn awaiting\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) candidateReviewPending(state *State, identity *IdentityState, candidate *VersionState) (GateSpec, reviewEvidenceSearch, bool) {\n\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[candidate.Kind]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn GateSpec{}, reviewEvidenceSearch{}, false\n\t}\n\tfor _, gateID := range kind.Gates {\n\t\tspec := s.Catalog.Gates[gateID]\n\t\tif spec.Class != \"review\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tkey := gateKey{Identity: identity.ID, Version: candidate.Seq, GateID: gateID}\n\t\tif latest := latestResult(state.GateResults[key]); The context is provenance only: it is\n// never folded into the plan content hash, so recording it changes no assembly\n// composition input and no pre-existing rule-free plan.\nfunc (s *Session) loweringContextValue() (map[string]any, bool) {\n\tctx := loweringRuleContext(s.Catalog.LoweringRules)\n\tif ctx.Empty() {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\treturn ctx.provenanceValue(), true\n}\n\nfunc headsDigest(state *State) string {\n\tvar parts []string\n\tfor _, id := range sortedIdentityIDs(state) {\n\t\tidentity := state.Identities[id]\n\t\tif identity.Head == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tparts = append(parts, id+\":\"+identity.Versions[identity.Head].ContentHash)\n\t}\n\tsum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(strings.Join(parts, \"\\n\")))\n\treturn hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])\n}\n\n// --- dispatch ---\n\nfunc (s *Session) dispatch(report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tstate, err := s.fold()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tactions := 0\n\tfor _, requestSeq := range sortedRequestSeqs(state) {\n\t\trequest := state.Requests[requestSeq]\n\t\tif request.Satisfied || request.Canceled || !request.PlanningActive() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif redirect, blocked := latestInapplicableRedirect(state, request.Seq); blocked {\n\t\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, fmt.Sprintf(\"dispatch withheld: redirect resolution %d exact artifact pin is no longer applicable\", redirect.Seq))\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tplan := state.Plans[request.Seq]\n\t\tif plan == nil || plan.SchemaVersion < 2 || plan.InvalidReason != \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor i := range plan.PlannedRuns {\n\t\t\tn, err := s.dispatchPlannedStep(state, request, &plan.PlannedRuns[i], report)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tactions += n\n\t\t\tif n > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t// Re-fold so later steps see the opened run.\n\t\t\t\tstate, err = s.fold()\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn actions, err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn actions, nil\n}\n\n// dispatchStep is retained for focused package tests. Production dispatch uses\n// dispatchPlannedStep so no work opens without an exact durable plan value.\nfunc (s *Session) dispatchStep(state *State, request *RequestState, step planStep, report *Report) (int, error) {\n\treturn s.dispatchStepResolved(state, request, step, report, nil, nil)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) prepareStep(state *State, request *RequestState, step planStep, report *Report) (preparedRun, bool, error) {\n\tvar prepared preparedRun\n\t_, err := s.dispatchStepResolved(state, request, step, report, &prepared, nil)\n\treturn prepared, prepared.Manifest != nil, err\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) dispatchPlannedStep(state *State, request *RequestState, planned *PlannedRun, report *Report) (int, error) {\n\tif planned == nil {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\treturn s.dispatchStepResolved(state, request, planned.Step, report, nil, planned)\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) dispatchStepResolved(state *State, request *RequestState, step planStep, report *Report, prepareOut *preparedRun, expected *PlannedRun) (int, error) {\n\tif redirect, blocked := latestInapplicableRedirect(state, request.Seq); blocked {\n\t\treport.Notes = append(report.Notes, fmt.Sprintf(\"dispatch withheld: redirect resolution %d exact artifact pin is no longer applicable\", redirect.Seq))\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\t// An unresolved escalation on this subject blocks fresh dispatch: the\n\t// Principal owns the next move, and churning attempts under a standing\n\t// refusal is capacity burn with no new information. An escalation whose\n\t// request is terminal is moot and vetoes nothing (State.Blocks).\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif state.Blocks(escalation) && escalation.BlockingScope == step.Subject {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif step.PassID == \"review\" && step.SubjectVersion != 0 && step.SubjectHash != \"\" {\n\t\tattempts := candidateBoundReviewRevisionsSinceLicense(state, step.Subject, step.SubjectVersion, step.SubjectHash)\n\t\tif attempts > s.Policy.RevisionBound {\n\t\t\treturn s.escalateReviewRevisionBounds(state, request, step, attempts)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// A resolved bounds escalation licenses fresh attempts: the Principal's\n\t// proceed means \"try again\", so only runs after the latest resolved\n\t// escalation naming this step count against the budget.\n\tvar licensedAfter uint64\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif escalation.Resolved && strings.Contains(escalation.Detail, step.StepID) &&\n\t\t\tescalation.Seq > licensedAfter {\n\t\t\tlicensedAfter = escalation.Seq\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tsubjectHead := state.HeadVersion(step.Subject)\n\tattempts, blocked := dispatchAttemptsSince(state, request.Seq, step.StepID, licensedAfter, s.Now(), subjectHead)\n\tif blocked {\n\t\treturn 0, nil // Open-Run Invariant or a durable scheduling retry horizon.\n\t}\n\t// A tripped budget escalates from whichever phase first observes it.\n\t// Under plan-v2 the prepare pass is the ONLY phase that ever sees a\n\t// trip \u2014 plan() excises tripped steps before dispatch can reach them \u2014\n\t// so a prepare-phase silent return made every budget and short-circuit\n\t// trip a silent zero-run plan with no adjudication hook (RQ-54904's\n\t// review step wedged exactly this way on 2026-07-12 after two identical\n\t// auth-era refusals). plan() runs inside the writing drive session and\n\t// escalateBounds self-dedupes, so emitting here is phase-safe and\n\t// idempotent; the tripped step still contributes no prepared run.\n\tif int(attempts) > s.Policy.RedispatchBound {\n\t\treturn s.escalateBounds(state, request, step, licensedAfter, false)\n\t}\n\t// Two consecutive allowlisted typed refusals under one sealed manifest\n\t// prove the next attempt doomed: escalate now carrying the signature\n\t// instead of burning the remaining budget (stalls-need-no-judgment@4,\n\t// clause six). A later resolution re-licenses attempts exactly as it does\n\t// for the exhausted bound.\n\tif attempts >= 2 && deterministicFailure(state, request.Seq, step.StepID, licensedAfter, subjectHead) {\n\t\treturn s.escalateBounds(state, request, step, licensedAfter, true)\n\t}\n\n\tpass, ok := s.Catalog.Passes[step.PassID]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"driver: pass %q not cataloged\", step.PassID)\n\t}\n\tvar revalidationEvaluation revalidationGateEvaluation\n\tif step.Revalidate {\n\t\tif subjectHead == nil || !subjectHead.Stale {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tkind, ok := s.Catalog.Kinds[subjectHead.Kind]\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tgateIDs := append([]string(nil), kind.Gates...)\n\t\tif len(step.RevalidationGateIDs) > 0 && !slices.Equal(step.RevalidationGateIDs, gateIDs) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tstep.RevalidationGateIDs = gateIDs\n\t\tstep.RevalidationKind = subjectHead.Kind\n\t\tevaluation, classified := s.evaluateRevalidationGates(state, step.Subject, subjectHead, kind)\n\t\tif !classified {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\trevalidationEvaluation = evaluation\n\t\tif evaluation.Judgment {\n\t\t\tstep.MinimumQuality = \"strong\"\n\t\t\tseen := map[string]bool{}\n\t\t\tfor _, class := range step.ExcludedAliasing {\n\t\t\t\tseen[class] = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Independence is an aliasing constraint. Backend quality remains\n\t\t\t// scheduler policy and is checked on the admitted evidence; it is not\n\t\t\t// encoded by excluding every class containing a baseline backend.\n\t\t\texcluded := []string{\"deterministic\", subjectHead.AliasingClass}\n\t\t\tfor _, class := range excluded {\n\t\t\t\tif class != \"\" && !seen[class] {\n\t\t\t\t\tseen[class] = true\n\t\t\t\t\tstep.ExcludedAliasing = append(step.ExcludedAliasing, class)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tseen := map[string]bool{}\n\t\t\tfor _, class := range step.ExcludedAliasing {\n\t\t\t\tseen[class] = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfor _, class := range s.tierZeroExcludedAliasingClasses() {\n\t\t\t\tif !seen[class] {\n\t\t\t\t\tseen[class] = true\n\t\t\t\t\tstep.ExcludedAliasing = append(step.ExcludedAliasing, class)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Gather inputs; The rejection reason, verbatim, is the binding steer \u2014 address it in full: \" + step.RejectionDetail + \" (the rejected version is pinned as diagnostic; preserve what the reason calls sound)\"\n\t}\n\tif step.CheckDetail != \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"revise \" + step.Subject + \": a mechanical check refused it or its downstream lowering \u2014 \" + step.CheckDetail + \"; the refused artifact is pinned as diagnostic; produce a corrected version that resolves the refusal\"\n\t}\n\treturn \"revise \" + step.Subject + \": \" + step.Reason + \"; inspect every pinned diagnostic artifact, address each substantive finding or refusal it carries, preserve sound work, and produce a candidate that resolves the named cause\"\n}\n\n// runSubjectPinMatches reports whether a prior run's sealed subject pin names\n// the same subject configuration the current step would run against. Attempts\n// against a superseded head neither consume the redispatch budget nor prove\n// the next attempt doomed: the world lawfully moved between them (RQ-57950,\n// escalations 63878/63890 \u2014 re-revalidations after admitted head movement\n// collapsed into \"the same deterministic failure\" under step-id-only\n// accounting). The pin is driver-sealed at run open, so a fresh budget always\n// costs a real admitted head movement \u2014 cosmetic churn cannot mint one. A\n// missing pin matches conservatively (legacy runs; produce steps whose\n// subject has no head yet).\nfunc runSubjectPinMatches(run *RunState, subjectHead *VersionState) bool {\n\tif run == nil || run.Manifest == nil {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tpin, ok := run.Manifest[\"subject_pin\"].(map[string]any)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\tseq := num(pin, \"version_seq\")\n\thash := str(pin, \"content_hash\")\n\tif seq == 0 && hash == \"\" {\n\t\treturn subjectHead == nil\n\t}\n\tif subjectHead == nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn seq == subjectHead.Seq && hash == subjectHead.ContentHash\n}\n\nfunc dispatchAttemptsSince(state *State, requestRef uint64, stepID string, licensedAfter uint64, now time.Time, subjectHead *VersionState) (int64, bool) {\n\tvar attempts int64\n\tretryPending := false\n\tfor _, run := range state.Runs {\n\t\tif run.StepID != stepID || run.RequestRef != requestRef {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif run.Open() {\n\t\t\treturn attempts, true\n\t\t}\n\t\tif run.SchedulingDeferred() {\n\t\t\t// A lane closure after this deferral changes the ledger-derived\n\t\t\t// in-flight set that caused saturation. Re-evaluate immediately;\n\t\t\t// retry_at remains the fallback when no capacity event intervenes.\n\t\t\tif run.RetryAt.After(now) && state.LastCapacityReleaseSeq <= run.SchedulingDeferralSeq {\n\t\t\t\tretryPending = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue // saturation defers; it never spends a redispatch attempt\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !runSubjectPinMatches(run, subjectHead) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif run.Seq > licensedAfter {\n\t\t\tattempts++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn attempts, retryPending\n}\n\nfunc (s *Session) escalateRevisionBounds(state *State, request %s\",\n\t\tstep.Subject, step.SubjectVersion, attempts, step.Reason)\n\tpayload := map[string]any{\n\t\t\"reason\": \"bounds_exhausted\", \"budget_kind\": \"revision\", \"detail\": planStep, licensedAfter uint64, shortCircuit bool) (int, error) {\n\tfor _, escalation := range state.Escalations {\n\t\tif state.Blocks(escalation) && strings.Contains(escalation.Detail, step.StepID) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tdetail := \"attempt budget exhausted for \" + step.StepID\n\tif shortCircuit {\n\t\tdetail = \"deterministic failure short-circuited the attempt budget for \" +
